# Baseline race reports — race-by-race detail reading

Each race is read the same way, without a favourite/outsider label. We say what we see and the result comes last. Order of reading: (1) race sky, (2) field and birth dates, (3) counts per partnership, (4) where natal meets sky, (5) field as a whole, (6) result.

S5 table columns, added during the race 6 and race 7 checks and back-filled for earlier races:

- **Exclusive on the sky?** A repeat is shared if any other chart in the race holds the same distance and coordinate, partner included.
- **Bodies.** Fast-body when a pair in the repeat includes Sun, Moon, Mercury, Venus or Mars (the pipeline's FAST set); slow when every body is a slow body or a fixed star. Asteroids count as slow under this rule. A slow repeat marks a birth period; a fast-body repeat is closer to a personal signature. For races 1 and 2 the label comes from the pairs listed in the report; where the pairs are not listed, the entry says so.

## Race 1 — Musselburgh 25/08/2021 13:45 (5 ran)

**Field**

| Cloth | Horse (foaled) | Jockey (born) |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | Major Snugfit (19 Mar 2016) | Paul Mulrennan (1982) |
| 2 | Inside Intel (31 Mar 2017) | Jason Hart (1994) |
| 3 | Yukon (17 Feb 2017) | Daniel Tudhope (1985) |
| 4 | Graces Quest (29 Jan 2018) | Andrew Mullen (1983) |
| 5 | Natural Value (13 Feb 2018) | Joe Fanning (1970) |

**Race sky**

- 74 TR-TR pairs at 80+. 17 of them involve Asc, MC, Vertex or PoF; 21 if PoS is counted too.
- Asc/PoF, Asc/PoS and Moon/Sun are all at 144.375. This is an identity (PoF = Asc + Moon − Sun), not three separate relationships.
- Ninths dominates: 26 RA Ninths and 11 Dec Ninths.
- Strongest pairs: Rahu/PoF Dec Whole Number 99.7 (gap 50.000), Eris/Fomalhaut RA Phi Powers 99.3, Jupiter/Fomalhaut RA Ninths 99.0, Ceres/Chiron RA Ninths 98.5.
- Busiest bodies: PoF 7; Asc, Pallas and Moon 6; Polaris, Transpluto, Quaoar, Gonggong and Mercury 5.
- Same-distance clusters:

  | Coordinate | Distance | Pairs |
  |---|---|---|
  | RA | 111.1 | Mars/Pleiades, Mars/Vega, Castor/Asc, Pallas/Sirius |
  | RA | 155.56 | Jupiter/Mercury, Ketu/Betelgeuse, Haumea/Sedna, Neptune/Vesta, Ketu/Vertex |
  | RA | 177.78 | Moon/Algorab, Mars/Pallas, Algol/Asc, Gonggong/Transpluto |
  | Dec | 11.11 | Juno/Saturn, Quaoar/Antares, Haumea/Alphecca |
  | Dec | 17.78 (160/9) | Eris/Aldebaran, Gonggong/Bellatrix, Mars/Pleiades |

- Transpluto/Alphecca (16.178) and Makemake/Vega (16.179) sit at the same Dec distance.

**Field structure: two near-twins.** Graces Quest and Natural Value were foaled 15 days apart.

- They share most of their S2 contacts at almost the same distances: Aldebaran/Rahu, Algorab/Ketu, Sirius/Ketu, Deneb Algedi/Ketu, Sirius/Jupiter, Algorab/Jupiter, Spica/Orcus, Pleiades/Sedna.
- They share these S1 contacts: Antares/Makemake, Spica/Uranus, Pleiades/Venus.
- Graces Quest has 0 unique certain S2 contacts and the lowest unique S3 in the field (65). Natural Value has the highest unique S3 (106).
- Major Snugfit is the only 2016 horse; Inside Intel and Yukon were both foaled in 2017.

**Counts per partnership** (H = horse, J = jockey)

| Partnership | S1 unique H/J | S5 H | S6-NN unique H/J | S6-TN H/J | A | B | D |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Snugfit / Mulrennan | 4/10 | 0 | 19/13 | 1/3 | 322 | 7 | 21 |
| Inside Intel / Hart | 8/13 | 0 | 12/16 | 1/2 | 325 | 11 | 25 |
| Yukon / Tudhope | 2/10 | 1 (J 1) | 19/18 | 1/1 | 317 | 8 | 28 |
| Graces Quest / Mullen | 6/10 | 2 | 18/12 | 3/3 | 324 | 7 | 29 |
| Natural Value / Fanning | 4/11 | 3 | 13/19 | 2/3 | 318 | 10 | 16 |

**Where natal meets sky**

- Natal repeats (S5) land on sky distances:
  - Graces Quest RA 177.778 (Alphecca–Sedna, Betelgeuse–Quaoar) matches the 177.78 sky cluster. Exclusive on the sky? No (Natural Value also holds 177.778). Bodies: slow.
  - Graces Quest Dec 15.5556 (Orcus–Procyon, Castor–Haumea) matches Juno/Midheaven Dec Ninths 15.54. Exclusive on the sky? Yes. Bodies: slow.
  - Natural Value RA 177.778 ×3 (Alphecca–Sedna, Castor–Pluto, Spica–Uranus) matches the same cluster. Exclusive on the sky? No (shared with Graces Quest). Bodies: slow.
  - Yukon Dec 22.222 (possible) matches Procyon/Asc Dec 22.221 (Ninths 97.8) and Pallas/Saturn Dec 22.235. Exclusive on the sky? Yes. Bodies: pairs not listed here.
  - Tudhope RA 166.667 (possible) matches Quaoar/PoF RA 166.598 (Ninths 94.1). Exclusive on the sky? Yes. Bodies: fast-body (Venus; Tudhope's pairs are Rigel–Vesta and Aldebaran–Venus, as in races 6 and 7).
  - Graces Quest is the only runner with two repeats, both certain and both on sky distances.
  - Natural Value also has two repeats with no sky match, both exclusive: Dec 4.444 and RA 0 (Conjunction).
- Polaris: 8 of the 21 unique S6-TN entries are transit→natal Polaris in RA. Mullen has 3 (Quaoar, Juno, Pallas), Natural Value 2 (Saturn, Eris), and Graces Quest, Mulrennan and Snugfit 1 each. Polaris is in 5 sky pairs.
- Mullen: transit Juno and transit Quaoar are themselves a sky pair (RA Sqrt2 96.8), and both hit his natal Polaris in RA Sqrt2 (95.5 and 92.3). Body, family and coordinate all match. No other runner has this.
- Graces Quest Saturn→Pleiades RA Whole Number 98.6 matches the sky pair Saturn/Pleiades RA Whole Number 85.0. For a 2018 horse natal Pleiades is nearly the transit Pleiades (about 0.05° of precession), so this is close to trivial.
- Alignment B contacts strong in both charts:
  - Snugfit/Mulrennan: Castor→Quaoar RA Ninths (H 96.4 fixed / J 73); Transpluto→Pluto (58/85).
  - Yukon/Tudhope: Ketu→Uranus TN Dec Golden Ratio (99/60); Algol→Juno RA Ninths (59/93).
  - Graces Quest/Mullen: all 7 B contacts are one-sided (one chart high, the other low).
- Three-way: only Inside Intel/Hart (Sun Dec 0.56, possible, loose). Eris at the midpoint of horse natal and the equator in 3 horses is a date constant. Neptune on the equator line is unique to Snugfit.

**Field as a whole.** The summary counts are tight (A 317–325). What differs is in the detail: natal repeats matching sky distances, the Polaris concentration, and strong two-sided B contacts.

**Result:** 1st Graces Quest (11/1, Mullen); 2nd Major Snugfit (5/1); 3rd Natural Value (11/8 favourite); 4th Yukon; 5th Inside Intel.

The sections above are the v2.1 count reading. The PINPOINT section below is read from the v2.2 rebuild.

### Race 1 — PINPOINT (engine v2.2, charts v2.3, natal at 12:00 standard time)

Read from pinpoint_reading v1.0. The overseeing check confirmed that no item below depends on a secondary family scoring 65–80 (the v1.1 `~` mark), so the ** and * marks here stand.

The race sky is unchanged (it is built at race time). Of the natal charts, only Mulrennan (Apr 1982), Inside Intel (31 Mar 2017) and Hart (Sep 1994) moved. Fanning (Ireland, Sep 1970) did not: in 1968–71 the UK and Ireland kept UTC+1 all year, which the time-zone database counts as standard time, so his noon is 11:00 UTC in both builds. Graces Quest, Mullen, Natural Value, Yukon, Tudhope and Major Snugfit were born in winter time and are unchanged.

"Full match" below means the sky pair's own family and coordinate (the script's **). "Sky family" means the same family in the other coordinate (*).

**Sky pairs grouped by shared body** (the groups that carry connections)

- Gonggong chain: Gonggong/Orcus RA Phi Powers 96.1, Ceres/Gonggong RA Ninths 94.2, Gonggong/Transpluto RA Ninths 93.3, Gonggong/Bellatrix Dec Ninths 91.9, Chiron/Gonggong RA Ninths 88.4, Mercury/Gonggong Dec Ninths 73.0 (sub-80, joins the group).
- Quaoar chain: Jupiter/Quaoar Dec Sqrt2 96.9, Juno/Quaoar RA Sqrt2 96.8, Quaoar/Alkaid RA Whole Number 96.4, Quaoar/PoF RA Ninths 94.1, Quaoar/Antares Dec Ninths 91.1, Quaoar/Algol RA Ninths 79.7 (sub-80).
- Chiron–Ceres: Ceres/Chiron RA Ninths 98.5, Chiron/Gonggong, Chiron/Transpluto RA Ninths 89.9.
- Jupiter–Fomalhaut–Eris: Jupiter/Fomalhaut RA Ninths 99.0, Eris/Fomalhaut RA Phi Powers 99.3, Eris/Polaris Dec Sqrt2 93.6, Eris/Procyon Dec Golden Ratio 98.0.
- Field-wide, no weight: Transpluto/Alphecca is carried as a link by all five partnerships, and every chart has a Transpluto hub. Birth-period / slow-body echo, set aside.

**1 Major Snugfit / 1 Paul Mulrennan**

- Eris/Polaris (Dec Sqrt2 93.6): Type 1 on the jockey, exclusive: N Eris→Polaris 63c and S Eris→Eris 98c, both full match. Horse S Eris→Eris RA Sqrt2 50c (sky family). Mulrennan also has both bodies onto natal Quaoar in Dec Sqrt2 (94c / 74c, full match).
- Jupiter/Fomalhaut (RA Ninths 99.0, number 3 pair): horse Type 2, both bodies onto natal Orcus, full match (95c / 95c, certain), exclusive.
- Orcus hub on the horse with Gonggong and Betelgeuse, so Orcus is on this horse too.
- Other exclusive horse items: Jupiter/Quaoar natal pair (Dec Ninths 96c); Quaoar/Antares Type 2 onto natal Uranus.

**2 Inside Intel / 2 Jason Hart**

- Haumea/Sedna (RA Ninths 89.2): a link, exclusive. Horse N RA Silver Ratio 87c; jockey X Dec Ninths 76c (sky family).
- Jupiter/Fomalhaut: Type 1 on the jockey (N Fomalhaut→Jupiter RA Ninths 90c, full match, + S Jupiter Dec Ninths 80p).
- Jupiter/Mercury: Type 1 on the jockey, every part possible.
- Ceres/Chiron (number 4 pair): horse Type 2 onto natal Rahu, full match (Ceres 96p, Chiron 100c).
- Hart has many small hubs (Ceres, Mercury, Gonggong, Bellatrix, Haumea, Sedna and others), each on 2–3 partners.

**3 Yukon / 3 Daniel Tudhope**

- Gonggong/Transpluto (RA Ninths 93.3): a link, full match on both charts (horse N 78c; jockey X 93c), exclusive as a link.
- Quaoar/Alkaid: Type 1 on the jockey (X RA Whole Number 98c, full match, + N 92c).
- Ketu/Mars (Dec Golden Ratio 94.0): horse Type 2 onto natal Uranus, full match (99c / 93c).
- Tudhope's Chiron: a hub with Transpluto (X RA Ninths 91c, full match), Sun and Arcturus (both sub-80 pairs), and Chiron/Gonggong both onto natal Transpluto (91c / 93c, full match). His Chiron self (RA Golden Ratio 88c) comes through Chiron/Part of Fortune, a sub-80 group pair (67.5), not a qualifying pair.

**4 Graces Quest / 4 Andrew Mullen**

- Gonggong/Orcus (RA Phi Powers 96.1): Type 1 on the horse (N Gonggong→Orcus RA Ninths 91c + X Orcus→Gonggong RA Phi Powers 78c, full match) and a link with the jockey (N Gonggong→Orcus Dec Phi Powers 65c, sky family). Exclusive to this partnership.
- Juno/Quaoar (RA Sqrt2 96.8): jockey Type 2 twice, full match, all certain, exclusive: both bodies onto natal Pluto (Juno 93c, Quaoar 96c) and onto natal Polaris (92c, 95c).
- Natal Pluto on Mullen is hit from three sky pairs, all certain and exclusive:

  - Chiron/Gonggong RA Ninths: TN Chiron→Pluto 100c and TN Gonggong→Pluto 98c (full match);
  - Gonggong/Bellatrix: TN Gonggong→Pluto RA Ninths 98c and NN Bellatrix→Pluto 83c. The sky pair is Dec Ninths, so this matches the family only.
  
  The Gonggong chain joins the horse's Gonggong/Orcus to the jockey's Pluto.
- Orcus: four routes into Graces Quest's natal Orcus, and a fifth on the jockey:
  - Gonggong/Orcus (the T1 above);
  - Asc/PoS both onto natal Orcus (86c, 91c), exclusive;
  - Ketu/PoS both onto natal Orcus, Dec Whole Number 71c / 74c (full match), exclusive;

- Quaoar/Alkaid (RA Whole Number 96.4): a link. Mullen X Quaoar→Alkaid 93c (full match), horse X 51c (full match). Inside Intel/Hart carry the same link, so not exclusive. It ties in with Mullen's Quaoar/Alkaid onto natal Orcus.
- Makemake/Vega (Dec Phi Powers 97.5): a link, exclusive as a partnership link. Horse N Makemake→Vega Dec Silver Ratio 81c; jockey N Dec Ninths 93p. Fanning also carries the natal pair (RA Golden Ratio).
- Saturn/Pleiades (RA Whole Number 85.0): a link, exclusive. Horse X Saturn→Pleiades RA Whole Number 99c (full match); jockey N Pleiades→Saturn RA Phi Powers 90c. For a 2018 horse natal Pleiades is close to transit Pleiades, so the horse side is close to trivial.
- Horse Chiron hub with Ceres (N Ceres→Chiron RA Ninths 66c, full match on the number 4 pair), Algorab, Arcturus and Algol, all Ninths or Whole Number. No other chart has it.

**5 Natural Value / 5 Joe Fanning**

- Juno/Quaoar: a link, but possible on both sides (horse N Dec Phi Powers 87p; jockey X Dec Sqrt2 86p).
- Sun/Castor (Dec Sqrt2 96.1): Type 1 on the horse, full match but possible and fast-body (N 79p, S Sun 83p).
- Jupiter/Quaoar: jockey Type 2 onto natal Juno, full match, possible (89p / 86p).
- Fanning's Eris/Procyon and Juno/Spica natal pairs are exclusive.

**Sweep additions** (sweeps 1–4 run 30/09 on the new v1.2 file, which adds section G; added, nothing above rewritten)

Quaoar then takes a transit pair on each chart, exclusive: the horse's natal Quaoar takes Rahu/PoF (TN + TN, 74c / 74c, certain) and Mullen's takes Orcus/Betelgeuse (TN Orcus 65c + NN Betelgeuse 79c, certain).   - Also exclusive: Arcturus/PoF onto the horse's natal Juno (NN 82c + TN 88c); Mulrennan's Quaoar hub (Algol, Neptune, PoF).
- 3 Yukon / Tudhope:
  - Section G adds three exclusive links: Chiron/Sun, T1 on one (horse X Chiron→Sun 60p; Tudhope N 92p + X Chiron→Sun RA Ninths 92c, full match), Pluto/Rigel (a mirror: horse X Pluto→Rigel 92c, transit; Tudhope N 97c, birth chart) and Saturn/Algol (N on both, birth chart, with Saturn hubs on both charts: horse Algol and Juno, Tudhope Algol and Pleiades, exclusive).
  - Natal Sun takes pairs on both charts, exclusive. Tudhope's takes three: Ceres/Chiron (TN Ceres 96p + TN Chiron 92c), Makemake/Polaris (TN 62p + NN 96c) and Altair/Vertex (NN 75p + TN 87p). The horse's takes Haumea/MC (72p / 78p) and Neptune/Vesta (93p / 74p). All possible on at least one side. With the Chiron/Sun G link and Tudhope's Sun hub (Chiron, Moon) and Moon hub (Sun, Transpluto), Chiron–Sun runs through both charts.
  - Also exclusive: the horse's Ketu hub (Betelgeuse, Procyon, Sun) and Mercury hub (Fomalhaut, Gonggong, Neptune); Makemake/Transpluto onto its natal Mars (possible); Pallas/Saturn onto Tudhope's natal Vesta (NN + NN, birth chart).
- 2 Inside Intel / Hart:
  - Section G: Mars/Haumea, exclusive, X on both (horse 72c; Hart 92p), transit. With the Haumea/Sedna link, the Haumea–Mars–Sedna group runs through both charts: the horse's Haumea self 48c and Haumea hub (Antares, Mars, Sedna); Hart's Mars (Haumea, Ketu), Haumea (Mars, Sedna) and Sedna (Haumea, Juno) hubs, all exclusive. Mars/Pleiades lands on Hart's natal Sedna (TN 79c + NN 63c) and Mars/Pallas on the horse's natal Ketu (TN 83c + NN 77p).
  - Three pairs land on a natal body on both charts, exclusive: Moon/Sun (horse natal Rahu, TN Moon 80c + NN Sun 81p; Hart natal Ketu, TN + TN 98c / 89c), Moon/Algorab (horse natal Chiron, TN 67c + NN 84c; Hart natal Ketu, TN 98c + NN 83c) and Chiron/Transpluto (horse natal Pallas and Sun, NN Chiron + TN Transpluto; Hart natal Pluto, TN + TN 71c / 90c). Hart's natal Ketu takes two Moon pairs, certain.
  - Three-pair bodies: the horse's natal Sun (Chiron/Gonggong NN + NN, Chiron/Transpluto NN + TN, Gonggong/Transpluto shared), mostly birth chart and possible; Hart's natal Transpluto (Eris/Fomalhaut NN + NN, Pallas/Saturn, Pallas/PoS shared) and natal Jupiter (Transpluto/Alphecca, Deneb Algedi/Vertex, Haumea/MC), mixed.
- 5 Natural Value / Fanning: two pairs land on both charts, exclusive unless noted: Gonggong/Bellatrix (horse natal Orcus, NN + NN, shared with Graces Quest; Fanning natal Sun, TN Gonggong 89c + NN Bellatrix 93p) and Haumea/Sedna (horse natal Juno, TN + TN 68c / 78p; Fanning natal Neptune, TN + TN 74c / 93c, certain). Juno–Quaoar hubs on both charts, exclusive: the horse's Juno self 57c and Juno hub (Chiron, Quaoar); Fanning's Juno (Quaoar, Spica), Quaoar (Alkaid, Juno) and Spica (Juno, Pallas) hubs. The Juno/Quaoar link itself is possible on both. Also exclusive: Mars/Pallas onto the horse's natal Quaoar (NN 93p + TN 99c) and Juno/Saturn onto Fanning's natal Uranus (NN 66p + TN 92c).
- Sweep 3: Chiron selves on Tudhope (RA Golden Ratio 88c, via a sub-80 pair, above) and Hart (Dec Whole Number 44p, via the sub-80 Chiron/Algol). None in Ninths.

**What the pinpoint reading shows (bodies and pairs, not how many)**

- Only Graces Quest/Mullen puts a certain Type 1 plus link (Gonggong/Orcus) on the Gonggong chain and then carries the chain into the jockey's chart: natal Pluto on Mullen is struck by Chiron/Gonggong and Juno/Quaoar (full match) and Gonggong/Bellatrix (family only), all certain and exclusive. Orcus is reached five ways across the partnership.
- Orcus also sits on Major Snugfit (Jupiter/Fomalhaut Type 2, Orcus hub), but Mulrennan's chart carries Eris/Polaris, not the Gonggong chain.
- Natural Value/Fanning's items are nearly all "possible" and fast-body.
- Every partnership has exclusive items, as the method warns. Graces Quest/Mullen's are the ones that thread one sky-pair group through horse and jockey with certain contacts.

**Against the result:** 1st Graces Quest (11/1, Mullen), 2nd Major Snugfit (5/1), 3rd Natural Value (11/8 favourite), 4th Yukon, 5th Inside Intel.

## Race 2 — Wolverhampton AW 25/08/2021 20:10 (8 ran)

Same day as race 1, 6h25m later.

**Field**

| Cloth | Horse (foaled) | Jockey (born) |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | Traveller (11 Feb 2014) | Harry Russell (1997) |
| 2 | Rain Gauge (14 Feb 2018) | David Egan (1999) |

| 4 | Calonne (28 Mar 2016) | Kieran Shoemark (1996) |
| 5 | Abnaa (20 Feb 2017) | Cam Hardie (1996) |
| 6 | Wow William (1 Mar 2018, France) | David Probert (1988) |
| 7 | Carpentier (17 Mar 2018) | Luke Morris (1988) |

**Race sky**

- 74 pairs at 80+. 9 involve Asc, MC, Vertex or PoF; 14 if PoS is counted too. Race 1 had 17 / 21.
- Ninths leads: 21 RA Ninths and 11 Dec Ninths.
- Strongest pairs: Venus/Pleiades RA Ninths 99.4, Makemake/Orcus RA Whole Number 99.2 (exactly 41.0), Chiron/Sun RA Ninths 98.7, Sirius/PoS Dec Sqrt2 98.5, Pallas/Regulus RA Phi Powers 98.4.
- Busiest bodies: Venus 8; Makemake and Mars 7; Eris and Gonggong 6. Moon is not prominent (it was 6 in race 1).
- Carried over from race 1 (the slow sky):
  - pairs: Eris/Fomalhaut, Makemake/Orcus, Pallas/Regulus, Haumea/Alphecca, Juno/Saturn, Quaoar/Antares;
  - Transpluto/Alphecca and Makemake/Vega again at an identical Dec distance (16.179 / 16.183);
  - clusters: RA 177.78 (Sun/Gonggong, Gonggong/Transpluto, Mars/Pallas), RA 155.56 (Ketu/Betelgeuse, Neptune/Vesta, Haumea/Sedna), RA 144.4–144.55 (Chiron/Sun, Procyon/MC, Pluto/Regulus, Chiron/Transpluto), RA 111.0 (Pallas/Sirius, Venus/Capella, Mars/Vega).
  - The Dec 11.11 cluster has grown to 5 pairs (Juno/Saturn, Haumea/Alphecca, Juno/Pallas, Chiron/Venus, Quaoar/Antares).
- New in race 2:
  - RA 133.33 (1200/9) cluster: Venus/Pleiades 133.338 (the top pair), Rahu/Vesta, Jupiter/PoF.
  - Dec 28.3 cluster: Venus/Pleiades, Procyon/MC, Eris/Fomalhaut.
  - The race 1 Dec 17.78 cluster is gone.

**Field structure: twins again**

- Cuban Cigar and Wow William were foaled on the same day, 1 Mar 2018 (GB and France, one hour apart at local noon). They are near-identical:
  - S1, S2 and S4 unique: 0 each; S3 unique: 2 and 1; S6-NN unique: 3 each.
  - Alignment B and D are 0 for both partnerships, because the unique measures cannot see them.
- Rain Gauge (14 Feb 2018) was foaled one day after race 1's Natural Value (13 Feb 2018). It carries the same RA 177.778 triple: Alphecca–Sedna, Spica–Uranus, Castor–Pluto.
- Five 2018 horses; Traveller is the only 2014 horse.

- Madame Tantzy has two certain repeats, both on sky distances involving the fast points (PoS, Vertex), and both exclusive in the field. Race 1's Graces Quest had the same shape: two certain repeats, both on sky distances.

- Horse version, Eris→Pallas TN Dec Ninths (pack 07): on Stunning Beauty (possible, mid 27.6) and Dalanijujo (possible, mid 0).
- Jockey version, Sun→Venus NN Dec Whole Number (pack 09, natal-to-natal): on Marco Ghiani (possible, mid 0), who rode Pretty In Grey, the only jockey holder. The horse Lola Showgirl also holds the NN contact (possible, mid 0).
- Madame Tantzy and Georgia Dobie carry neither.

**Alignment B contacts strong in both charts**

- Madame Tantzy/Dobie: Chiron→Vega NN Dec Whole Number (H 97.5 / J 96.2), the strongest two-sided contact in the four races so far; Moon→Mars TN RA Phi Powers (72/83); near-miss Uranus→Chiron TN Dec Whole Number (75/58).
- Dalanijujo/Marquand: Mars→Ketu TN Dec Ninths (73/90.5).
- Stunning Beauty/Cherchi: near-miss Uranus→Haumea TN RA Whole Number (67/81).
- Lola Showgirl/Pearson: near-miss Juno→Chiron TN Dec Sqrt2 (98.6/66).
- Pretty In Grey/Ghiani: all one-sided (Pallas→Transpluto 29/98, Quaoar→Neptune 34/98).

**Three-way and equator**

- Madame Tantzy/Dobie: Quaoar Dec 0.066, transit at the midpoint of horse and jockey (cross-chart, certain, closeness 34.4, unique), the only true three-way in the race. Also equator hits for Ceres (0.134) and Sun (0.254), both possible and loose.
- Lola Showgirl/Pearson: Neptune Dec 0.072, transit at the midpoint of horse natal and the equator (certain, closeness 28.1).
- The other three partnerships have none.
- Eris does not appear in the three-way/equator results this time; the thread from races 1–3 is broken.

**Field as a whole**

- A runs 333–351, and the lowest A is Madame Tantzy/Dobie.
- The three April 2017 foals blank each other, and the date-constant rule strips the TN layer to 7 entries.
- One partnership stands apart in the detail: Madame Tantzy/Dobie, the only horse outside the 2017 group. It has the most individual S2, two certain exclusive repeats on fast-point sky distances, the strongest two-sided B, the most TN (Dobie 3), the only true three-way, and neither RV-1 "didn't win" contact.
- Marquand's chart meets this sky again, at the 144.44 cluster and at the Dec 17.78 cluster (0.003).

**Result:** 1st Madame Tantzy (12/1, Georgia Dobie); 2nd Dalanijujo (11/4, Tom Marquand); 3rd Stunning Beauty (5/2 favourite); 4th Lola Showgirl (3/1); Pretty In Grey (100/30) unseated rider.

**Races 1–4 — what we see**

- Second outsider winner, after Graces Quest (11/1) in race 1. Both winners carried two certain natal repeats on sky distances. Both winners' jockeys carry TN weight: Mullen 3 Polaris in race 1, Dobie the most TN (3, one Polaris) here.
- The winning favourites (races 2 and 3) came from quiet partnerships. The losing favourite here, Stunning Beauty/Cherchi, is also quiet (0/0 TN, B 3), so quietness alone does not separate them. Stunning Beauty does carry the RV-1 horse "didn't win" contact Eris→Pallas Dec Ninths.
- Near-twins in all four races. For the first time none of the near-twin group won: the winner is the one runner outside every birth group, horse and jockey alike.
- Fast pairs: race 1 17 (outsider won), race 2 9 (favourite), race 3 22 (favourite), race 4 12 (outsider). No ordering by count yet.
- Tom Marquand across two races: his repeats met both skies. He was 3rd on Karr and 2nd on Dalanijujo.
- The Dec 17.78 cluster has grown to 5 pairs over the three days.
- Eris is absent from the three-way results for the first time.

**What the pinpoint reading shows**

- Madame Tantzy/Dobie is the only partnership where the same bodies, Sun and Neptune, run through horse and jockey on exclusive links:
  - Sun: Sun self in RA Phi Powers on both charts through Sun/Vega and Sun/Sedna, with a Sun/Sedna Type 1 on the horse. The horse's Sun hub is shared with Marquand.
  - Neptune: the Neptune/Saturn link, with exclusive Neptune hubs on both charts, and two pairs (Eris/Saturn, Chiron/Venus) landing on Dobie's natal Neptune.

  Phi Powers runs through the Sun side (Sun/Vega RA, Sun/Sedna Dec, Sun self RA on both charts) and Dobie's side of Neptune/Saturn. The horse's side of Neptune/Saturn is Silver Ratio (a natal pair at 83c).
- Chiron self in Ninths (the original jockeys' thread) is on two charts here, each inside a Type 1:
  - Marquand: Chiron/Antares Type 1, full match, 55c.
  - Stunning Beauty: Chiron/Venus Type 1, full match, 52c.

  Dobie's Chiron self is in Silver Ratio (67c, full match).
- Stunning Beauty/Cherchi has the most Type 1s and exclusive links in the field, plus many full-match Type 2s.

**Against the result:** 1st Madame Tantzy (12/1, Georgia Dobie); 2nd Dalanijujo (11/4, Tom Marquand); 3rd Stunning Beauty (5/2 favourite, Cherchi); 4th Lola Showgirl (3/1); Pretty In Grey (100/30) unseated rider.

- The winner is the partnership with Sun and Neptune mirrored on both charts through exclusive links.
- Chiron self in Ninths on a Type 1 sat on 2nd (Marquand) and 3rd (Stunning Beauty), not the winner. This is a counter-example for the check-in, as in race 3.
- The partnership with the most Type 1s and exclusive links was the favourite and finished 3rd.

- New Dynasty/Levey: Sun and Venus, both possible and loose.

**Field as a whole**

- A runs 330–349. The field is a single birth year, so the horse side is thin throughout; Northern Express and Cooperation have no unique S2.
- The partnership detail is spread out: Rogue Bear has the most repeats and a two-sided B; Cooperation/Villiers has the Quaoar three-way plus a two-sided Moon→Mars (the race 4 winner's shape); Moore has two genuine TN sky matches and a certain repeat on the sky; the Polaris concentration is back (7/20).

**Result:** 1st Dark Shift (100/30 favourite, Ryan Moore); 2nd Papacito (4/1); 3rd May Night (12/1); 4th Rogue Bear (15/2); 5th Cooperation (11/2, Dark Shift's near-twin); 6th New Dynasty (33/1); 7th Helm Rock (9/2); 8th Northern Express (5/1).

- Pack 04 (Transpluto→Mercury Dec Sqrt2) is on the 2nd (Papacito), 3rd (May Night) and 8th (Northern Express); the winner does not hold it. This matches RV-1's "didn't win, often placed".
- Packs 07 and 09 are both on the winning favourite's partnership here, the opposite of RV-1, where no winner held them.

- Eris is back in the equator results, on Helm Rock.

- The winner is the only T1 × T1 partnership and the only one with natal Gonggong hit on both charts; its T1 × T1 is carried by the jockey, with the horse's side through a secondary family.
- Shepherd's Chiron self in Silver Ratio (97c, certain Type 1) finished 4th.
- The partnership with the most exclusive links (Helm Rock/De Sousa) finished 7th. Northern Express/Spencer, with two exclusive full-match natal-pair links, finished last.

Also exclusive: Tudhope's Betelgeuse hub (Gonggong, Saturn).

**What the pinpoint reading shows**

- Golden Flame/Fanning is the only partnership with Ketu/Saturn on both charts, as T1 on one: the horse's Saturn self (91c) and Fanning's Saturn→Ketu natal pair (98c, with X 80c full match). Fanning's natal Ketu also takes both bodies of Saturn/Regulus. Ketu/Saturn also reaches Atzeni's natal Neptune (95c / 90c), Dawson's natal Orcus (89c / 94c), Kingscote's natal Vesta (98c / 79p) and Probert's natal Mercury (possible).
- Chiron self in Ninths is on one chart only: Praiano, inside its Chiron/Vertex Type 1 (48c, sky family, certain). The other Chiron selves (Kingscote 70c, Tudhope 94c, RA Phi Powers) are shared and come only through sub-80 pairs.
- Tynwald/Tudhope holds the only T1 × T1 (Saturn/Transpluto, certain on both), on a pair that is a natal pair on five horses.
- Contact/Dawson has the most exclusive links (three), with Uranus self on both charts.

**Against the result:** 1st Golden Flame (9/1, Joe Fanning); 2nd Praiano (3/1, Atzeni); 3rd Valley Forge (11/8 favourite, Probert); 4th Contact (13/2, Dawson); 5th Roseabad (15/2, Kingscote); 6th Tynwald (14/1, Tudhope).

- The winner carries Ketu/Saturn on both charts, certain, and no other partnership does.
- The only Chiron self in Ninths finished 2nd (Praiano); its jockey's natal Neptune takes the winner's pair.
- The favourite (Valley Forge/Probert) has two exclusive links, Mars/Rahu and Chiron/Juno, with most elements possible; the winner's Ketu/Saturn is certain on both sides.
- The only T1 × T1 (Tynwald/Tudhope) finished last. Its pair, Saturn/Transpluto, is a natal pair on five horses (2018 birth-period). The partnership with the most exclusive links (Contact/Dawson) finished 4th.

## Race 7 — Redcar 14/09/2021 13:10 (8 ran)

10 days after race 6.

**Field**

| Cloth | Horse (foaled) | Jockey (born) |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | May Blossom (2 Apr 2019, GB) | Daniel Tudhope (1985) |
| 2 | Le Beau Garcon (10 Apr 2019, GB) | Paul Mulrennan (1982) |
| 3 | Chant For More (25 Apr 2019, Ireland) | Kevin Stott (1994) |
| 4 | Firebomb (3 Apr 2019, GB) | Graham Lee (1975) |
| 5 | Suanni (2 Apr 2019, GB) | Tom Eaves (1981) |

The first 2-year-old race: all eight horses were foaled between 11 Mar and 25 Apr 2019. Three race 1 jockeys return (Mulrennan, Tudhope, Hart); Tudhope also rode in race 6.

**Race sky**

- 79 pairs at 80+. 14 involve Asc, MC, Vertex or PoF; 17 if PoS is counted too.
- Ninths leads with 31 pairs (19 RA, 12 Dec). Then Sqrt2 14, Silver Ratio 11, Phi Powers 8, Whole Number 7, Golden Ratio 7, Conjunction 1.
- Strongest pairs: Jupiter/Betelgeuse RA Ninths 99.9 (122.223), Moon/Pallas RA Phi Powers 99.3, Chiron/Neptune Dec Phi Powers 99.2, Sedna/Transpluto Dec Ninths 99.2 (2.222), Gonggong/Betelgeuse Dec Ninths 99.15 (18.890), Mercury/Rahu RA Ninths 99.1 (133.326).
- Busiest bodies: Chiron 8; Sedna and Pluto 7; Neptune and Orcus 6.
- Identity: Asc/PoF = Moon/Sun = 98.399.
- Same-distance clusters:

  | Coordinate | Distance | Pairs |
  |---|---|---|
  | RA | 177.78 | Neptune/MC 177.801, Pleiades/Asc 177.915, Ceres/Antares 177.995 |
  | RA | 155.56 | Ceres/Quaoar 155.571, Uranus/Makemake 155.578, Capella/Asc 155.616, Chiron/Arcturus 155.445 |
  | RA | 144.44 | Rahu/Alkaid 144.410, Pluto/Regulus 144.249, Ceres/Arcturus 144.549 |

  | Dec | 28.3 | Saturn/Altair 28.145, Rahu/Alkaid 28.290, Transpluto/Vega 28.327 |

- The Dec 17.78 cluster is still there, but its members have changed: Gonggong/Bellatrix and Eris/Aldebaran have dropped out, and Chiron now holds two of the pairs.
- Still present from early September: Pluto/Regulus, Quaoar/Algol, Rahu/Vega, Chiron/Antares, Saturn/Algorab, Transpluto/Vega, Pluto/Pleiades.

**Field structure: true twins, a near-triple, and a second near-twin pair**

- May Blossom and Suanni were foaled on the same day (2 Apr 2019, both GB), and Firebomb one day later. May Blossom and Suanni are zero on every unique horse measure (S1, S2, S3, S5, S6-NN, S6-TN); Firebomb is almost zero (S1 1, S5 1, NN 13). May Blossom/Tudhope and Suanni/Eaves both have B 0 and D 0.
- Macon Belle (28 Mar) and Van Zant (29 Mar) are one day apart: both have S3 certain unique 0, and Macon Belle has no unique S2.
- Le Beau Garcon (10 Apr) sits outside both groups. Its unique S2: Castor/Rahu, Betelgeuse/Sun, Arcturus/Pallas, Altair/Sun.
- Queen Spirit (11 Mar), the earliest foal, has the most certain unique S2 of the horses (5; 10 including possible), mostly Venus, Pallas and Juno declination: Sirius/Venus, Algorab/Venus, Deneb Algedi/Venus, Altair/Pallas, Altair/Juno. Le Beau Garcon has 2, Chant For More 1.
- Chant For More (25 Apr) is the latest foal.
- The date-constant line is 4 of 8; 38 TN date constants are removed (22 horse, 16 jockey).

Le Beau Garcon has the highest horse NN (24) and 0/0 TN. Chant For More/Stott has the highest D certain (16); Queen Spirit/Rodriguez the highest B (9).

**Where natal meets sky: natal repeats (S5) against sky distances**

"Bodies" is slow when both bodies of every pair in the repeat are slow bodies or fixed stars, and fast-body when a pair includes Sun, Moon, Mercury, Venus or Mars (the pipeline's FAST set). A slow repeat marks a birth period; a fast-body repeat is closer to a personal signature.

| Repeat | Holder(s) | Sky match | Exclusive on the sky? | Bodies |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| RA 133.333 | Eaves (Antares–Eris fixed, Jupiter–Pleiades; certain) | Mercury/Rahu 133.326 (0.007), the number 6 sky pair (99.1) | yes | slow |
| Dec 15.556 | Le Beau Garcon (Orcus–Procyon 94.7, Altair–Makemake 91.7; certain) | Betelgeuse/Vertex Dec Ninths 15.547 (0.009) | yes | slow |
| RA 166.667 | Tudhope (Rigel–Vesta, Aldebaran–Venus; possible) | Juno/Sedna 166.622 (0.044) | yes | fast-body (Venus) |
| RA 111.111 | Chant For More (Jupiter–Regulus, Uranus–Vega; certain) | Antares/PoS 110.960 (0.151, loose; that pair is Phi Powers) | yes | slow |
| RA 177.778 | May Blossom, Suanni, Firebomb, Le Beau Garcon (all certain) | Neptune/MC 177.801 (0.023), Pleiades/Asc | no (4 charts) | slow |
| RA 144.444 | May Blossom, Suanni, Firebomb, Le Beau Garcon ×3, Chant For More ×3, Stott, Queen Spirit (all certain) | Rahu/Alkaid 144.410 (0.035), Ceres/Arcturus, Pluto/Regulus | no (7 charts) | slow (Stott's includes Arcturus–Mars) |
| RA 61.803 (Phi Powers) | Graham Lee (Quaoar–Vega, Regulus–Uranus; certain) | no 80+ sky match | — (exclusive) | slow |
| RA 179.443 (Phi Powers) | Queen Spirit (Pluto–Procyon, Castor–Pluto; certain) | no 80+ sky match | — (exclusive) | slow |

- Le Beau Garcon's Dec 15.556 uses Orcus–Procyon, the same pair as race 1 Graces Quest's exclusive Dec 15.556 (Orcus–Procyon, Castor–Haumea). Orcus is a slow body and Procyon a fixed star, so this distance marks a birth period (Graces Quest was foaled Jan 2018, Le Beau Garcon Apr 2019). It is exclusive within this field, but it is not a personal signature the way a fast-body repeat would be. The exclusive-repeat tally should not count birth-period repeats the same as personal ones.
- In this race every exclusive sky-matched repeat is slow except Tudhope's 166.667 (one pair uses Venus).
- Tudhope's RA 166.667 has now landed on a sky distance in three races (1, 6, 7); it is his natal chart.
- The 2019 horses share 177.778 and 144.444 widely: the 2019 birth-period version of the marker (Alphecca–Sedna itself is not among these repeats).

**S6-TN (21 unique entries)**

- Polaris: 4 of 21 (Tudhope Mars→Polaris and Gonggong→Polaris, Chant For More Orcus→Polaris, Hart Vertex→Polaris).
- Sirius: 4 (Chant For More Neptune→Sirius, Lee Orcus→Sirius, Hart Quaoar→Sirius 99.3, Rodriguez Pluto→Sirius). Arcturus: 1 (Chant For More).
- Jason Hart has 5, the most. Three are body + family + coordinate matches with sky pairs, all genuine (born 1994): Sedna→Bellatrix Dec Ninths 97.7 (sky 96.7), Mercury→Rigel Dec Sqrt2 91.6 (sky 91.65), Pluto→Fomalhaut RA Sqrt2 91.2 (sky 87.0).
- Both sides of one partnership: Chant For More Saturn→Altair RA Silver Ratio 91.7 (fixed) and Kevin Stott 95.4 (fixed), a full match with the sky pair Saturn/Altair RA Silver Ratio 98.1 (the number 9 pair). Genuine for Stott, close to trivial for the 2019 horse. Chant For More also carries Pluto→Arcturus Dec Golden Ratio (sky 85.0) and Neptune→Sirius RA Silver Ratio (sky 89.0): full matches, but trivial for a 2-year-old.
- Rodriguez Pluto→Sirius Dec Phi Powers 96.6: full match with sky 84.7 (born 1997).
- 0 unique TN: Le Beau Garcon/Mulrennan (both sides), May Blossom, Firebomb, Suanni, Van Zant/Sullivan (both sides), Macon Belle.

**RV-1 cross-reference (all "possible")**

- Pack 04, horse TN Transpluto→Mercury Dec Sqrt2: Chant For More (mid 0), Macon Belle (mid 0), Queen Spirit (max 13.5). Jockey Tudhope too.
- Pack 07, horse TN Eris→Pallas Dec Ninths: Firebomb (max 31.5), Macon Belle (mid 0). Jockeys Stott and Rodriguez (mid 28.3) too.
- Pack 09, jockey NN Sun→Venus Dec Whole Number: Tudhope, Sullivan, Rodriguez (mid 13.0). The horse Le Beau Garcon also holds the NN contact.
- RV-1 lay measures: horse unique TN ≥ 1 is only Chant For More (4, one certain) and Queen Spirit (2, one certain). The lowest unique NN among horses is the twins May Blossom and Suanni (0).

**Alignment B contacts strong in both charts**

- None reach 70 on both sides. Near-misses on Queen Spirit/Rodriguez: Neptune→Polaris NN RA Golden Ratio (69/74), Orcus→Sedna TN RA Ninths (64/98), Venus→Mercury TN RA Ninths (86/63).
- May Blossom/Tudhope, Suanni/Eaves and Van Zant/Sullivan have no B contacts at all. Le Beau Garcon/Mulrennan and Firebomb/Lee have one each, both one-sided (Firebomb/Lee Altair→Mars 49/98).

**Three-way and equator (all "possible" this time)**

- Macon Belle/Hart: Mercury Dec 0.043, transit at the midpoint of horse and jockey (closeness 57.4); Mars RA 1.02 (loose).
- Chant For More/Stott: Pallas Dec 0.026, jockey natal at the midpoint of transit and the equator (closeness 74.1).
- Le Beau Garcon/Mulrennan: Sun Dec 0.076 and Rahu 0.085, horse natal at the midpoint of transit and jockey (closeness 24.5 and 15.2); Mercury equator 0.112.
- Firebomb/Lee: Chiron 0.091, transit at the midpoint of horse and jockey. Suanni/Eaves Pallas 0.22. May Blossom/Tudhope Mercury equator 0.13.
- No Eris and no Quaoar three-way.

**Field as a whole**

- A runs 332–343. A single-crop field of 2-year-olds with a same-day twin pair, so much of the horse side is empty.
- Le Beau Garcon/Mulrennan: the horse outside both birth groups, with the highest horse NN, an exclusive certain repeat at 0.009 from a sky pair (slow bodies, a birth-period distance), D certain 10, and quiet on TN (0/0) and B (1).
- Chant For More/Stott has the most D certain and a full-match TN on both sides. Jason Hart has the most genuine TN sky matches. Eaves has an exclusive certain repeat on the number 6 sky pair (0.007, slow), but his horse Suanni is a blank twin.

**Result:** 1st Le Beau Garcon (9/4 favourite, Paul Mulrennan); 2nd Firebomb (3/1); 3rd May Blossom (9/2); 4th Suanni (12/1); 5th Queen Spirit (22/1); 6th Chant For More (100/1); 7th Macon Belle (100/30); 8th Van Zant (18/1).

**Races 1–7 — what we see**

- Twins: the same-day/next-day group (Firebomb, May Blossom, Suanni) finished 2nd, 3rd and 4th, and the winner is the horse outside every birth group, as in race 4. In races 1, 2, 3 and 5 one twin won.
- Winning favourites (races 2, 3, 5, 7) were quiet on TN: Le Beau Garcon/Mulrennan 0/0, Cuban Cigar/Ryan 0/0, Toussarok/Murphy 0/1, Dark Shift/Moore 0/4. All four horses have 0 unique TN: RV-1 pack 01/02 again ("winning favourites quiet on horse TN").
- Le Beau Garcon differs from the earlier winning favourites in having an exclusive, certain, sky-matched repeat (Dec 15.556, 0.009). Before this, none had one (Cuban Cigar's was shared, Toussarok's possible, Dark Shift's had no match). But it is a slow, birth-period repeat, the same Orcus–Procyon distance Graces Quest carried in race 1.
- Jockey TN sky matches are not enough on their own: Hart had 3 genuine matches and finished 7th on the 100/30 second favourite, while Moore (2 matches, race 5) and Murphy (1 match, race 3) won.
- The same full-match TN on both sides of a partnership: race 2 Duchess Rose/Hamilton 8th, race 3 Invincible Larne/Callan 5th (not a sky match), race 6 Golden Flame/Fanning 1st, race 7 Chant For More/Stott 6th.
- Pack 04 holders finished 5th, 6th and 7th; this time none was placed.
- Tudhope's RA 166.667 repeat met the sky in races 1, 6 and 7 (4th, 6th, 3rd).
- Fast pairs by race: 17 O, 9 F, 22 F, 12 O, 14 F, 12 O, 14 F.

### Race 7 — PINPOINT (engine v2.2, charts v2.3, natal at 12:00 standard time; pinpoint v1.1)

The race sky is unchanged. Ten natal charts moved (summer births; UK clocks changed on 31 Mar 2019):
- Horses: May Blossom and Suanni (both 2 Apr 2019), Firebomb (3 Apr), Le Beau Garcon (10 Apr) and Chant For More (25 Apr, Ireland).
- Jockeys: Mulrennan (22 Apr 1982), Sullivan (28 May 1987, Ireland), Rodriguez (4 May 1997), Stott (Jul 1994, Denmark) and Hart (27 Sep 1994).

Van Zant (29 Mar), Macon Belle (28 Mar), Queen Spirit (11 Mar), Tudhope, Lee and Eaves are unchanged.

May Blossom and Suanni share a foaling date, so their horse charts are identical. Anything on one of them is shared with the other and is never exclusive.

"Full match" = the sky pair's own family and coordinate (**); "sky family" = same family, other coordinate (*); `~` = a match only through a secondary family scoring 65–80, read like a sub-80 pair.

**Sky pairs grouped by shared body**

- Chiron: Chiron/Neptune Dec Phi Powers 99.2 (secondary Dec Ninths 65.4), Chiron/Spica Dec Golden Ratio 91.2, Chiron/Orcus Dec Ninths 91.0, Chiron/Arcturus RA Ninths 89.5, Chiron/Pallas RA Golden Ratio 87.2, Chiron/Antares RA Whole Number 85.6, Chiron/Procyon Dec Sqrt2 84.1, Chiron/Sedna RA Whole Number 80.4.
- Sedna: Sedna/Transpluto Dec Ninths 99.2, Sedna/Bellatrix Dec Ninths 96.7, Juno/Sedna RA Ninths 96.2, Makemake/Sedna Dec Sqrt2 94.6, Pluto/Sedna RA Phi Powers 93.8, Mars/Sedna Dec Silver Ratio 89.6.
- Rahu–Mercury: Mercury/Rahu RA Ninths 99.1, Rahu/Alkaid RA Ninths 96.3, Rahu/Vega Dec Ninths 88.8, Mercury/Saturn Dec Silver Ratio 90.0, Mercury/Betelgeuse RA Whole Number 87.6.
- Haumea: Haumea/MC RA Golden Ratio 96.3, Haumea/Aldebaran Dec Ninths 95.3, Haumea/Spica Dec Silver Ratio 93.9, Sun/Haumea Dec Silver Ratio 93.0.
- Ceres: Ceres/Quaoar RA Ninths 98.5, Ceres/PoF RA Sqrt2 94.5, Ceres/Arcturus RA Ninths 88.9, Ceres/Antares RA Ninths 83.0.
- Pallas: Moon/Pallas RA Phi Powers 99.3 (number 2 pair), Pallas/Vesta Dec Silver Ratio 91.8, Chiron/Pallas.
- Betelgeuse: Jupiter/Betelgeuse RA Ninths 99.9 (top pair), Gonggong/Betelgeuse Dec Ninths 99.2, Betelgeuse/Vertex Dec Ninths 91.9.
- Field-wide, no weight:
  - Transpluto/Vega is a link on all eight partnerships, and every chart has a Transpluto hub.
  - Orcus/Transpluto is a link on four partnerships.
  - Natal pairs on most 2019 horses: Pluto/Capella, Pluto/Sirius, Pluto/Pleiades and Orcus/Transpluto, with matching Pluto and Orcus hubs (birth-period sharing).

**1 May Blossom / 1 Daniel Tudhope**

- Ketu/Transpluto (RA Sqrt2 92.2): a link, exclusive (horse N Transpluto→Ketu 53c, full match; Tudhope N Dec Phi Powers 95c).
- The horse's chart is identical to Suanni's, so it has no exclusive item of its own.
- Tudhope, exclusive:
  - Natal Vesta is hit by four Dec Silver Ratio pairs, all full match: Sun/Haumea (93c / 86c), Haumea/Spica (86c / 81c), Sun/Spica (93c / 81c) and Sun/Orcus (93c / 89c).
  - Natal Quaoar is hit by Mercury/Rahu (77c / 98c) and Rahu/Alkaid (98c / 92c), RA Ninths, full match.
  - Mercury/Saturn onto natal Transpluto (94c / 95c).
  - Gonggong/Betelgeuse onto natal Jupiter (sky family, 90p / 83c).
  - His Chiron/Neptune onto natal Pluto is `~`.

**2 Le Beau Garcon / 2 Paul Mulrennan**

- No exclusive link among pairs at 80 or above. The only such pair on both charts is the field-wide Transpluto/Vega. No Type 1 on Mulrennan.
- Pallas on the horse, exclusive:
  - Pallas/Vesta Type 1: X Vesta→Pallas Dec Silver Ratio 81p + S Pallas Dec Silver Ratio 50p (both full match, both possible).
  - A Pallas hub with Moon, Vesta, Mars and Algol that no other chart has. It includes Moon/Pallas, the number 2 pair (X Moon→Pallas RA Phi Powers 46c, full match).

- Mars/Pallas (RA Ninths 76.2, sub-80) is on both charts, seen only in the hubs:

**Sweep additions** (sweeps 1–4 run 30/09 on the new v1.2 file, which adds section G; added, nothing above rewritten)

- 2 Le Beau Garcon / Mulrennan: section G confirms Mars/Pallas as T1 on one, the only partnership (horse N 87c + X 89p, full match; Mulrennan X 55p). Pluto/Transpluto (G, T1 on one) is shared with Macon Belle. One addition: Makemake/PoS onto the horse's natal Mars (TN 62c + TN 79p), exclusive, so Mars is also a natal body on the horse.
- 6 Van Zant / Sullivan: a qualifier the section above lacks. Sullivan's natal Chiron takes the three Ceres pairs through his own natal Ceres (NN Ceres→Chiron 96c each time): Ceres/Arcturus and Ceres/Antares are NN + NN (birth chart) and only Ceres/Quaoar has a transit side (TN Quaoar 96c). So that convergence is mostly birth chart. His natal Venus takes three pairs (Jupiter/Castor, Saturn/Altair, Vega/Asc), all possible. Chiron/Orcus lands on both charts (horse natal Sun, TN + TN 99p / 73p, possible; Sullivan natal Transpluto, above). Also exclusive: the horse's Ceres hub (Antares, Quaoar); Sullivan's Antares hub (Chiron, Vesta) and Quaoar/Transpluto onto his natal Ceres (possible).
- 1 May Blossom / Tudhope: section G adds two exclusive mirrors: Mars/Polaris (horse N Mars→Polaris 93p, birth chart; Tudhope X Mars→Polaris RA Sqrt2 94c, full match, transit) and Saturn/Procyon (horse N Procyon→Saturn 89c, full match, birth chart; Tudhope S Saturn 86p, full match, transit). Mercury/Rahu and Rahu/Alkaid land on both charts (the horse's natal Uranus, shared with its twin Suanni; Tudhope's natal Quaoar, above, exclusive).
- 5 Suanni / Eaves: the same two Rahu pairs land on Eaves's natal Vesta (Mercury/Rahu TN + TN 69p / 78p; Rahu/Alkaid TN 78p + NN 89p), exclusive, possible. So Mercury/Rahu and Rahu/Alkaid reach the twin horses' shared natal Uranus and a different natal body on each twin's jockey (Tudhope Quaoar, certain; Eaves Vesta, possible). Eaves's natal Vesta takes a third pair (Ceres/Quaoar, NN 64p + TN 61c) and his natal Neptune three (Ceres/Antares NN + NN, birth chart; Sedna/Bellatrix TN 78c + NN 60c; Juno/Moon, possible). Also exclusive: Eris/Vertex onto his natal Orcus (TN + TN, 70c / 83c).
- 4 Firebomb / Lee (no exclusive link), read in full: Sedna reaches natal bodies on both charts. Horse: Chiron/Sedna onto natal Haumea (TN + TN, 88c / 70c) and Makemake/Sedna onto natal Ketu (TN 64c + TN 94p). Lee: Makemake/Sedna onto natal Chiron (96c / 91c, above) and Sedna/Bellatrix onto natal Sun (possible). Two pairs land on both charts: Makemake/Sedna (horse Ketu; Lee Chiron) and Betelgeuse/Vertex (horse natal Orcus, above; Lee natal Chiron, NN 63c + TN 63c). So Lee's natal Chiron takes two pairs, certain, exclusive.
- 3 Chant For More / Stott: section G adds Betelgeuse/Asc, exclusive, X on both (91c / 90c, full match, transit, certain). The horse's natal Uranus takes three pairs: Orcus/PoF (TN + TN, 78c / 76c), Betelgeuse/Vertex (NN 99p + TN 91c) and Chiron/Pallas (TN 62p + TN 73c), mixed. Also exclusive: the horse's Venus self 91p and Vega hub (Rahu, Transpluto); Capella/Asc onto Stott's natal Haumea (NN 83c + TN 62c).
- 7 Macon Belle / Hart: section G adds Eris/Saturn, T1 on one, the only partnership (horse X Saturn→Eris 92c + S Eris 68c; Hart X Eris→Saturn 97c), transit and certain on both. Saturn runs on through both charts: the horse's Saturn self 63c; on Hart, Pallas/Vesta onto natal Saturn (above) and Saturn/Altair onto natal Haumea (TN 92c + NN 75c). Also exclusive: Jupiter/Castor onto Hart's natal Eris (TN 64c + NN 62c) and Uranus/Castor onto the horse's natal Mars (possible).
- 8 Queen Spirit / Rodriguez: Ceres/PoF lands on both charts (horse natal Mars, TN + TN 60p / 65p, possible; Rodriguez natal Neptune, TN + TN 73c / 68c, certain), exclusive. The section G Sun/Sirius is shared with Van Zant.
- Sweep 3: the only Chiron self is Firebomb's (RA Sqrt2 78p), confirmed.

**What the pinpoint reading shows**

- Le Beau Garcon/Mulrennan has no exclusive link among pairs at 80 or above. What it carries alone:
  - Natal Sedna hit on both charts, certain and full match (horse by Orcus/PoF and Pluto/Arcturus, Mulrennan by Haumea/Aldebaran).
  - A Pallas thread on the horse: Pallas/Vesta Type 1, Pallas self, a Pallas hub including the number 2 pair, and Uranus/MC onto natal Pallas. It crosses to Mulrennan only through the sub-80 pair Mars/Pallas (horse 87c / 89p full match; Mulrennan 55p, sky family).
  - Chiron/Neptune (number 3 pair) onto Mulrennan's natal Ketu, 98c / 96c.
- There is no Chiron self in Ninths in this race. The only Chiron self is Firebomb's (RA Sqrt2 78p, sky family).
- The strongest Chiron-in-Ninths items are natal pairs, not selves:
  - Eaves's natal Chiron→Orcus Dec Ninths 99c (full match).
  - Sullivan's natal Chiron, taken by three Ceres pairs in RA Ninths.
- Van Zant/Sullivan has the most exclusive links (four).
- May Blossom and Suanni have identical horse charts. What separates those two partnerships is on the jockeys.

**Against the result:** 1st Le Beau Garcon (9/4 favourite, Paul Mulrennan); 2nd Firebomb (3/1, Graham Lee); 3rd May Blossom (9/2, Tudhope); 4th Suanni (12/1, Eaves); 5th Queen Spirit (22/1, Rodriguez); 6th Chant For More (100/1, Stott); 7th Macon Belle (100/30, Hart); 8th Van Zant (18/1, Sullivan).

- The winner has no exclusive link among pairs at 80 or above. It is the only partnership with natal Sedna hit on both charts, and it carries the horse's Pallas thread, which reaches the jockey only through sub-80 Mars/Pallas (possible on his side).
- The partnership with the most exclusive links (Van Zant/Sullivan) finished last.
- Of the twin horse charts, May Blossom/Tudhope (3rd) finished ahead of Suanni/Eaves (4th).

## Race 8 — Yarmouth 15/09/2021 15:20 (5 ran)

The day after race 7, about 26h later.

**Field**

| Cloth | Horse (foaled) | Jockey (born) |
|---|---|---|
| 2 | Chance (29 Feb 2016, GB) | Jack Mitchell (Dec 1988) |
| 3 | United Front (14 Feb 2017, USA) | Josephine Gordon (1993) |
| 4 | Ropey Guest (23 Feb 2017, GB) | Tom Queally (1984) |
| 5 | Ajyaall (3 May 2017, France) | Tom Marquand (1998) |
| 6 | Dejame Paso (20 Mar 2018, Ireland) | William Buick (Jul 1988) |

Returning jockeys: Tom Marquand's third ride (races 3 and 4), William Buick (race 3).

**Race sky**

- 78 pairs at 80+. 16 involve Asc, MC, Vertex or PoF; 17 if PoS is counted too.
- Ninths leads with 38 pairs (25 RA, 13 Dec), the most Ninths so far. Then Whole Number 12, Sqrt2 10, Phi Powers 7, Silver Ratio 5, Conjunction 3, Golden Ratio 3.

- Busiest bodies: Ceres 9; Neptune and Vertex 7; Chiron, Jupiter and Venus 6.
- Identity: Asc/PoF = Moon/Sun = 114.362.
- Same-distance clusters:

  |---|---|---|
  | RA | 133.33 | Mars/Algol 133.351, Venus/Capella 133.290, Quaoar/Algol 133.263, Fomalhaut/MC 133.225, Ceres/Pluto 133.184 (5 pairs) |

  | RA | 155.56 | Venus/Pleiades 155.590, Uranus/Makemake 155.620, Chiron/Arcturus 155.403, Ceres/Quaoar 155.725 |

  | Dec | 17.78 | Chiron/Spica 17.7785 (number 2 pair), Chiron/Orcus 17.782, Rahu/Vega 17.770, Pallas/Aldebaran 17.793; Pallas/Saturn 17.999 (Whole Number) |
  | Dec | 11.11 | Sun/Rigel 11.108, Vertex/PoS 10.999, Ceres/Alphecca 11.018 |

- Carried over from race 7 (a day earlier), among others: Rahu/Alkaid (now the top pair), Chiron/Spica, Chiron/Orcus, Rahu/Vega, the RA 122.22 trio (Neptune/Procyon, Saturn/Algorab, Jupiter/Betelgeuse), Uranus/Makemake, Ceres/Quaoar, Ceres/Arcturus, Ceres/Antares, Chiron/Arcturus, Quaoar/Algol, Sedna/Transpluto, Sedna/Bellatrix, Quaoar/Bellatrix, Orcus/Spica, Pluto/Sedna, Pluto/Sirius, Neptune/Altair, Gonggong/Betelgeuse.

**Field structure: a near-twin pair and two birth-period sets**

- United Front (14 Feb 2017) and Ropey Guest (23 Feb 2017) are near-twins, 9 days apart. Ropey Guest's unique S2 is small (Altair/Venus, Alphecca/Vesta, Deneb Algedi/Mercury; 2 certain). United Front's: Procyon/Mars, Betelgeuse/Ceres, Procyon/Vesta, Betelgeuse/Venus, Fomalhaut/Pallas, Deneb Algedi/Sun (3 certain).
- Chance (29 Feb 2016, a leap-day foal) was foaled 5 days after race 4 winner Madame Tantzy (24 Feb 2016) and carries her unique S2 set: Bellatrix/Uranus, Procyon/Vesta, Procyon/Jupiter, Bellatrix/Jupiter, Bellatrix/Sedna (plus Rigel/Sun, Algorab/Mercury, Sirius/Mercury, Bellatrix/Vesta, Procyon/Pallas). A Feb-2016 birth-period set.
- Dejame Paso (20 Mar 2018) is the only 2018 horse, so the early-2018 nodal set is unique to it here: Sirius/Ketu, Castor/Ceres, Algorab/Ketu, Aldebaran/Rahu, Sirius/Jupiter, Deneb Algedi/Ketu, Algorab/Jupiter (shared between the twins in races 1 and 3).
- Jockeys Mitchell and Buick were both born in 1988.
- The date-constant line is 3 of 5; 51 TN date constants are removed (30 horse, 21 jockey).

**Counts per partnership** (H = horse, J = jockey)

- Marquand has two exclusive fast-body repeats landing 0.001 from the top two sky pairs, the closest matches in eight races.
- Ropey Guest's one repeat (slow) is shared with Marquand. Chance, United Front and Dejame Paso have no repeats.
- Gordon and Buick meet the 177.78 cluster with the same distance but different pairs.

**S6-TN (21 unique entries)**

- Tom Queally has 7. Four are body + family + coordinate matches with a sky pair, all genuine (born 1984): Asc→Algorab RA Sqrt2 94.8 (sky 80.9), Vertex→Betelgeuse Dec Golden Ratio 91.8 (sky 92.7), Rahu→Vega Dec Ninths 91.3 (sky 93.5, in the Dec 17.78 cluster), Vesta→Spica RA Ninths 90.7 (sky 88.4). The other three: PoF→Sirius, Vesta→Procyon, Gonggong→Polaris.
- Jack Mitchell 4, two of them full matches: Vertex→Algol RA Sqrt2 91.6 (sky 98.3, the number 6 pair) and Venus→Sirius RA Ninths 90.6 (sky 88.5).
- Marquand 3: Pluto→Sirius Dec Phi Powers 92.8 (full match with sky 97.7, the number 10 pair; the same contact Rodriguez carried in race 7), Orcus→Sirius Dec Ninths (full match, sky 86.3), Saturn→Capella.
- Buick 2: Saturn→Alphecca Dec Whole Number 90.5 (full match with sky 90.7). Ajyaall has Saturn→Alphecca in RA Golden Ratio (bodies only).
- Polaris: 4 (United Front Moon→Polaris, Queally Gonggong→Polaris, Dejame Paso Asc→Polaris and Jupiter→Polaris). Sirius: 4 (Mitchell, Queally, Marquand ×2). Arcturus: 2 (Gordon Vertex→Arcturus, Buick Vesta→Arcturus).
- No unique TN: Chance, Ropey Guest.

**RV-1 cross-reference (Yarmouth is in packs 03 and 05)**

- Pack 05 (horse S6-NN unique count): Ropey Guest is top on its own, 24 against Ajyaall's 22.
- Pack 03 (jockey NN Neptune→Sirius RA Ninths): William Buick holds it at 99.4 and finished 4th. This is the same contact as his RA 177.778 repeat (Neptune–Sirius 99.4, Algorab–Mars 99.2), so the pack 03 holding and his sky-matched repeat are one natal distance.
- Pack 04 (horse TN Transpluto→Mercury Dec Sqrt2, all possible): United Front (mid 24.0), Ropey Guest (mid 0), Ajyaall (mid 32.7), Dejame Paso (mid 0). Four of five horses hold it, above the 3-of-5 line, so it is a date constant here. Only Chance lacks it.
- Pack 07 (horse TN Eris→Pallas Dec Ninths): no holders.
- Pack 09 (jockey NN Sun→Venus Dec Whole Number, possible): Mitchell (mid 1.2), Buick (mid 37.1). The horse Ajyaall also holds the NN contact.
- RV-1 lay measures: horse unique TN ≥ 1 is United Front 1, Ajyaall 1 (fixed), Dejame Paso 2. Lowest horse unique NN: Chance and Dejame Paso (11 each).

**Alignment B contacts strong in both charts**

- Chance/Mitchell has three, the most for one partnership in eight races: Fomalhaut→Orcus NN RA Ninths (78/81), Neptune→Ketu NN RA Ninths (82/99), Gonggong→Juno TN RA Ninths (98/86). Near-miss Moon→Neptune TN Dec Ninths (89/64).
- Ropey Guest/Queally: Vega→Vesta NN RA Ninths (81/94).
- Dejame Paso/Buick (14 B, the most): near-misses only, Algol→Quaoar (90/56), Algorab→Haumea (68/69).
- United Front/Gordon and Ajyaall/Marquand: all one-sided.

**Three-way and equator**

- Eris equator on the near-twins, transit Eris at the midpoint of horse natal and the equator: United Front Dec 0.010 (certain, closeness 89.6) and Ropey Guest Dec 0.030 (certain, closeness 70.0). 2 of 5 horses, below the date-constant line, so shared but not a date constant.
- Ropey Guest/Queally also: Jupiter Dec 0.052, horse natal at the midpoint of transit and the equator (certain, closeness 48.5, unique); Uranus 0.085 (possible).
- Chance/Mitchell: Saturn Dec 0.043, horse natal at the midpoint of transit and jockey (certain, closeness 57.3, unique).
- United Front/Gordon: Ceres 0.14 (possible). Dejame Paso/Buick: Juno 0.075 (possible). Ajyaall/Marquand: none.

**Field as a whole**

- A runs 318–330.
- Marquand holds the closest sky contact in eight races: two exclusive fast-body repeats at 0.001 from the top two pairs, plus 3 TN (two full matches).
- Queally has the most TN of any chart so far (7, four genuine full matches). His horse Ropey Guest has one shared slow repeat, 0 TN, the highest horse NN (top on its own, the pack 05 measure), a unique certain Jupiter equator hit, and the Eris equator hit shared with its near-twin.
- Chance/Mitchell has three two-sided B contacts and a certain Saturn three-way. Dejame Paso/Buick has the most B.

**Result:** 1st Ropey Guest (9/1, Tom Queally); 2nd United Front (18/1, Ropey Guest's near-twin); 3rd Ajyaall (6/4 favourite, Tom Marquand); 4th Dejame Paso (5/2); 5th Chance (3/1).

**Races 1–8 — what we see**

- Fourth outsider winner, and the near-twins finished 1st and 2nd. One twin has won in races 1, 2, 3, 5 and 8; in races 4 and 7 the winner was outside the birth groups.
- This outsider winner has no exclusive sky-matched repeat: Ropey Guest's only one is slow and shared with Marquand. Madame Tantzy and Golden Flame had both exclusive; Graces Quest one exclusive and one shared.
- Exclusive fast-body repeats are not enough on their own: Marquand's two are the closest sky contacts in eight races, and he finished 3rd on the 6/4 favourite. Across three rides: 3rd, 2nd, 3rd.
- Jockey charts with 3 or more unique TN, races 1–8 (winners in bold):

  | Race | Jockeys with 3+ TN (finish) |
  |---|---|
  | 1 | Mulrennan 3 (2nd), **Mullen 3 (1st)**, Fanning 3 (3rd) |
  | 2 | Hamilton 5 (8th) |
  | 3 | Doyle 4 (4th), Callan 3 (5th) |
  | 4 | **Dobie 3 (1st)** |
  | 5 | Spencer 4 (8th), **Moore 4 (1st)**, Shepherd 3 (4th) |
  | 6 | Atzeni 4 (2nd), **Fanning 3 (1st)** |
  | 7 | Eaves 3 (4th), Lee 3 (2nd), Hart 5 (7th) |
  | 8 | Mitchell 4 (5th), **Queally 7 (1st)**, Marquand 3 (3rd) |

**What the pinpoint reading shows**

- An Tailliur/O'Neill: the Sun–Rahu–Eris–Ceres group (Ceres/Eris, Eris/Rahu, Ceres/Rahu) converges on one natal body on each chart: Makemake on the horse (certain) and Sun on the jockey. Its three exclusive links are all in Ninths and run through selves on the horse (Chiron, Ceres, Pallas), mostly possible; O'Neill's X Ceres→Sun is certain (77c). The horse carries the only exclusive Chiron self in RA Ninths (59p, possible), linked to the jockey through Chiron/Vesta. Ketu and Orcus hub on both charts.
- Khan/Reed carries the strongest single Type 1: Ceres/Eris on the horse, certain and full match. The same group runs into natal bodies on both charts, spread across two (horse Ceres, Reed Pluto). An Tailliur/O'Neill and Khan/Reed are the only partnerships sharing the Eris/Rahu link.
- Story Of Friends/Gillard has Gonggong/Antares onto natal Makemake on both charts, certain.
- Get An Oscar/Bowen has the only T1 × T1 (Rahu/Sun, mostly possible) and the most exclusive links (eight).
- Terrierman's natal Mars takes six pairs from the Sun–Rahu–Eris–Ceres group.

**Against the result:** 1st An Tailliur (6/5 favourite, Jonjo O'Neill Jr); 2nd Khan (3/1, Reed); 3rd Get An Oscar (12/1, Bowen); 4th Terrierman (9/1, Sheppard); 5th Story Of Friends (8/1, Gillard); 6th Gavrocheka (7/1, O'Brien).

- The winning favourite's group pairs converge on one natal body on each chart (horse Makemake, certain; jockey Sun). Its links are in Ninths, mostly possible.
- It is the first baseline winner whose Chiron self in Ninths is exclusive and carries a link to the jockey (Chiron/Vesta). Toussarok (race 3) also had one (RA Ninths 57p), but it was shared with Karr and Healing Power.
- Khan/Reed (2nd) also runs the group into natal bodies on both charts, but spread across Ceres and Pluto rather than one body per chart.
- Story Of Friends/Gillard, with one pair onto the same natal body on both charts (Gonggong/Antares onto Makemake), finished 5th.
- The only T1 × T1 and the most exclusive links (Get An Oscar/Bowen) finished 3rd.
- Of the near-twins, An Tailliur won and Gavrocheka finished last.

"Full match" = the sky pair's own family and coordinate (**); "sky family" = same family, other coordinate (*); `~` = a match only through a secondary family scoring 65–80, read like a sub-80 pair. - 3 Pens Man / O'Neill: the horse's Jupiter hub (Arcturus, Moon), Procyon hub (Neptune, Pallas) and Ceres hub (Neptune, Sun), and Arcturus/PoS onto its natal Jupiter (NN 85c + TN); O'Neill's Bellatrix hub (Jupiter, Transpluto), Gonggong/Arcturus onto natal Jupiter (possible) and Jupiter/Saturn onto natal Vesta (NN + NN, birth chart, as in race 11). All exclusive.
- Sweep 3: the only Chiron self is O'Neill's (Dec Silver Ratio 81c), confirmed.

**What the pinpoint reading shows**

- The Sun–Rahu–Eris–Ceres group from race 11 is still in the sky and converges on a single jockey natal body on four partnerships: Houlihan's Chiron (certain), O'Neill's Sun (again), O'Brien's Neptune and Brace's Pluto (certain).

Eris/Rahu and Ceres/Eris land on a natal body on both charts.
- Forget You Not/Best carries a different group: Rahu with Makemake and Uranus, on two exclusive links (Rahu/Makemake 41c / 45c; Rahu/Uranus 56c / 79c), with Rahu hubs on both sides. The horse converges on natal Venus (possible); the jockey carries Rahu as a hub and a Uranus self, not a natal body.
- No Chiron self in Ninths in the race; the only Chiron self is O'Neill's (Silver Ratio).
- Blaze A Trail/Brace has the most exclusive links at 80 or above (three).

**Against the result:** 1st Forget You Not (25/1, James Best); 2nd Jarlath (11/1, Houlihan); 3rd Caspers Court (5/2, Scudamore); 4th Pens Man (5/4 favourite, O'Neill Jr); Pointed And Sharp (11/2, O'Brien) and Blaze A Trail (10/1, Brace) pulled up.

- In race 11 the three-pair group convergence on both charts was on the winner (6/5). In race 12 it is on Jarlath, 2nd at 11/1, beaten by a 25/1.
- The winner carries the Rahu–Makemake–Uranus thread through both charts as exclusive links and hubs, running into the horse's natal Venus (possible).
- O'Neill's natal Sun took the same three group pairs in both races on the card: 1st on An Tailliur, 4th on Pens Man. An Tailliur carried the group onto natal Makemake (certain); Pens Man carries only Ceres/Rahu (`~`) onto natal Pluto.
- The two partnerships with both the jockey convergence and exclusive links at 80 or above (Pointed And Sharp/O'Brien, Blaze A Trail/Brace) both pulled up.

Ace Rothstein, Napper Tandy, Hogan and Probert are winter births. Near-twins: Roseabad and Bascule (10 and 8 Apr 2018, two days apart); most of Roseabad's horse items are shared with Bascule. Hollie Doyle also rode in race 3; Probert in races 2, 5 and 6. The zip also held a second race's files from the same day folder (`_630`); I read only the `_1830` files.

"Full match" = the sky pair's own family and coordinate (**); "sky family" = same family, other coordinate (*); `~` = a match only through a secondary family scoring 65–80, read like a sub-80 pair. Section G = sub-80 group pairs (65–80, sharing a body with a qualifying pair) carried by horse and jockey. Birth chart = N / NN (natal pairs); transit = X, S, TN (the sky reaching the chart).

**Sky pairs grouped by shared body**

- Rahu: Rahu/Haumea Dec Ninths 99.0, Pallas/Rahu RA Ninths 98.8 + RA Sqrt2 83.9, Rahu/Capella RA Sqrt2 98.6, Moon/Rahu RA Whole Number 94.2 (and Ketu/Moon 94.2), Rahu/Vesta RA Silver Ratio 87.4.

- Field-wide, no weight: Transpluto/Capella is a link on all six partnerships. Also broad: Quaoar/Sedna is a link on four (Roseabad, Napper Tandy, Liverpool Knight, Bascule, a horse natal pair on all four); Gonggong/Arcturus is a natal pair on five horses, with a Gonggong hub on five.

- 3 Ace Rothstein / Hogan: two natal bodies take a pair on both charts, exclusive, certain:
  - Natal Quaoar: horse Mercury/Gonggong (TN Mercury 74c + NN Gonggong 89c); Hogan Sedna/Arcturus (TN Sedna 84c + NN Arcturus 80c). The section G Gonggong/Quaoar link is N on both (89c / 89c, birth chart); Hogan's Quaoar hub (Gonggong, Sedna) is exclusive.
  - Natal Pluto: horse Mars/Altair (TN Mars 79c + NN Altair 72c); Hogan Neptune/PoS (TN + TN, 88c / 91c). The section G Neptune/Pluto link is horse S Neptune 80c, Hogan X Neptune→Pluto 85c (transit on both).
  - Also exclusive, not above: horse Pluto/Transpluto onto natal Vesta (NN + NN, birth chart); Hogan Castor/Vertex onto natal Ketu (NN 74c + TN 90c), Neptune/Alkaid onto natal Jupiter (TN 94c + NN 94p), Quaoar/Asc onto natal Orcus (possible), Venus self 40p.
- 2 Marshall Plan / James Doyle's Mercury/Gonggong sweep adds nothing new. 6 Bascule / Rooke's thread was added at the overseer's check. 4 Napper Tandy and 5 Liverpool Knight have no exclusive A or G link.

**What the pinpoint reading shows**

- One group runs through both charts as transit on one partnership: Rahu–Pallas–Capella on Ace Rothstein/Hogan (two exclusive links, X on both, hubs on both, Pallas/Rahu onto Hogan's natal Mars). It does not converge on one natal body on the horse.
- Juno runs through both charts of Roseabad/Hollie Doyle, mostly as birth chart (N hubs on both; the one transit is the horse's X Orcus→Juno). Gonggong runs through Marshall Plan/James Doyle as a horse hub (mixed N / X) and two Gonggong pairs onto Doyle's natal Transpluto (transit).
- One exclusive pair lands on the same natal body on both charts: Pluto/Orcus onto natal Uranus on Liverpool Knight and Harley. The horse side is transit and certain (TN + TN, 95c / 96c); Harley's side is half birth chart and possible (TN Pluto 94p + NN Orcus 76c). The horse's natal Uranus takes three pairs from three groups.
- Pluto, Transpluto and Orcus run through both charts of Bascule/Rooke as transit, certain: the Pluto/Transpluto link (selves on both), then natal Orcus on the horse (Pluto/Asc) and natal Transpluto on Rooke (Haumea/Orcus), both exclusive.
- Three-pair bodies: Liverpool Knight's natal Uranus (three groups, mixed c/p) and Ace Rothstein's natal Transpluto (one transit, Moon/Rahu, and two birth-chart NN pairs). No body takes three pairs from one sky group.
- Chiron self in Ninths: Ace Rothstein (RA Ninths 76c, full match, exclusive, with the Chiron/Alphecca Type 1). The other Chiron selves are not Ninths: Hollie Doyle (RA Silver Ratio 49p), Liverpool Knight and Rooke (RA Sqrt2 56p / 55p, shared), Rooke (RA Whole Number 79p).
- Ace Rothstein/Hogan has the most exclusive links (two at 80 or above, four in section G).

**Against the result:** 1st Bascule (85/40 favourite, George Rooke); 2nd Marshall Plan (4/1, James Doyle); 3rd Liverpool Knight (100/30, Harley); 4th Roseabad (12/1, Hollie Doyle); 5th Napper Tandy (11/2, Probert); 6th Ace Rothstein (7/1, Hogan).

- The favourite winner carries the Pluto–Transpluto–Orcus body group through both charts, transit and certain: the exclusive Pluto/Transpluto link (selves on both), landing on the horse's natal Orcus and Rooke's natal Transpluto (both exclusive). Rooke has the Chiron/Eris Type 1 with Chiron and Eris hubs. Most of the horse's items are shared with the near-twin Roseabad, which finished 4th.
- The partnership with the most exclusive links (Ace Rothstein/Hogan, the Rahu–Pallas–Capella transit thread and the Chiron self in Ninths) finished last at 7/1.
- The one exclusive same-body landing on both charts (Pluto/Orcus onto natal Uranus, Liverpool Knight/Harley; transit and certain on the horse, half birth chart and possible on Harley) finished 3rd at 100/30.
- Marshall Plan/James Doyle, with the Gonggong thread (horse hub, two pairs onto Doyle's natal Transpluto), finished 2nd.

**What the pinpoint reading shows**

- Three pairs from one sky group onto one natal body, certain, exclusive, on three charts:

  - The horse Larllunna's natal Uranus: three Neptune–Midheaven–Vertex pairs, all TN + TN, transit.
  - Stammers' natal Ketu: the Eris–Quaoar–Bellatrix triangle (TN + TN and TN + NN Bellatrix), full match.
- Other bodies with three or more pairs: Russell's natal Saturn (two Neptune pairs and Pluto/PoS, certain, half birth chart on the Neptune pairs); Marquand's natal Vesta (three Neptune–Midheaven–Vertex pairs, mixed c/p) and natal Venus (mostly possible); Heart Throb's natal Juno (four pairs, all possible); Larllunna's natal Venus and Beasley's natal Pallas (possible or birth chart).

  - Poco Contante/Gormley: Pluto–Orcus–Neptune through two exclusive links and Gormley's Pluto and Neptune hubs, ending on natal Orcus on each chart by different pairs (horse NN Neptune + TN Midheaven, certain; Gormley Ceres/Eris `~`).
  - Larllunna/Russell: Neptune–Midheaven–Vertex onto the horse's natal Uranus (transit, certain) and two Neptune pairs onto Russell's natal Saturn (TN Neptune + NN star, certain).
  - Silver Bubble/Marquand: Gonggong hubs on both charts and a transit pair onto natal Gonggong on each chart (horse Eris/Saturn, certain; Marquand Juno/Rahu, certain). The two exclusive links are birth chart.
  - Gold Terms/Stammers: Eris–Quaoar–Bellatrix, birth chart on the horse (Eris/Bellatrix N 83c) with Quaoar pairs onto its natal Mars (possible on the transit side), and the full triangle onto Stammers' natal Ketu (certain).
  - Rabat/Beasley: Juno, transit, on both (horse Juno/Sun onto natal Uranus; Beasley Juno/Rahu Type 1 and Juno/Sedna onto natal Neptune), certain.
- Exclusive links at 80 or above: Poco Contante/Gormley and Heart Throb/Lee three each; Silver Bubble/Marquand two (both birth chart); Larllunna/Russell one; Gold Terms/Stammers and Rabat/Beasley none.
- No Chiron self on any chart in this race.

**Against the result:** 1st Heart Throb (9/1, Graham Lee); 2nd Rabat (Evens favourite, Beasley); 3rd Silver Bubble (11/5, Marquand); 4th Larllunna (33/1, Russell); 5th Gold Terms (11/2, Stammers); 6th Poco Contante (200/1, Gormley).

- The winner carries Juno through both charts, in Ninths: the Neptune–Midheaven–Vertex group runs into the horse's natal Juno (three pairs, full match, possible, exclusive), Juno carries on to Lee as a Juno self Dec Ninths 92c (sky family, certain) through the sub-80 Juno/Castor link (section G, exclusive), and Neptune/Midheaven also lands on Lee's natal Rahu (sky family, certain). Possible on the horse, certain on the jockey. Its three exclusive links at 80 or above (Sun/Bellatrix, Mercury/Pluto, Ceres/Moon) are separate and mostly birth chart.
- The favourite, 2nd, had no exclusive link; its thread is Juno, transit and certain on both charts (horse Juno/Sun onto natal Uranus; Beasley Juno/Rahu Type 1 and Juno/Sedna onto natal Neptune).
- The three single-group, three-pair convergences finished 4th (Larllunna's natal Uranus, 33/1), 5th (Stammers' natal Ketu) and last (Gormley's natal Uranus, 200/1).
- Poco Contante/Gormley, joint most exclusive links with the Pluto–Orcus–Neptune thread ending on natal Orcus on both charts, finished last at 200/1.

## Race 16 — Newbury 26/11/2021 13:50 (5 ran)

### Race 16 — PINPOINT (engine v2.2, charts v2.3, natal at 12:00 standard time; pinpoint v1.2)

**Field**

| Cloth | Horse (foaled) | Jockey (born) |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | Solo (14 Feb 2016, France) | Harry Cobden (5 Nov 1998) |
| 2 | Mister Coffey (30 Apr 2015, France) | James Bowen (12 Mar 2001, Wales) |
| 4 | Fifty Ball (30 Apr 2015, France) | Jamie Moore (31 Jan 1985) |
| 5 | Cascova (18 May 2015, Ireland) | Daryl Jacob (25 Aug 1983, Ireland) |
| 7 | Not Available (1 May 2015, Ireland) | Stan Sheppard (11 Sep 1997) |

Boothill (3) and L'Homme Presse (6) did not run. All charts are at 12:00 standard time. Six are summer births (a note only of which charts differ from clock time): Mister Coffey, Fifty Ball, Cascova, Not Available, Jacob and Sheppard. Solo, Cobden, Bowen and Moore are winter births. Identical charts: Mister Coffey and Fifty Ball (both 30 Apr 2015, France), so no horse-side item on either is exclusive. Near-twin: Not Available (1 May 2015, Ireland) shares many horse items with them. The zip also held a second race's files from the same day folder (`_150`); I read only the `_1350` files.

"Full match" = the sky pair's own family and coordinate (**); "sky family" = same family, other coordinate (*); `~` = a match only through a secondary family scoring 65–80, read like a sub-80 pair. Section G = sub-80 group pairs (65–80, sharing a body with a qualifying pair) carried by horse and jockey. Birth chart = N / NN (natal pairs); transit = X, S, TN (the sky reaching the chart).

- Horse, other exclusive items: Mercury/Venus Type 1 (N 87c + S Mercury 44p) with a Mercury hub (Chiron, Venus); Pallas/Pleiades, Pluto/Procyon and Saturn/Transpluto natal pairs; Procyon hub (Ketu, Mars, Pluto) and Transpluto hub (Capella, Saturn); natal Jupiter takes Ketu/Uranus (TN + TN, 94c / 93c) and Ketu/Castor (TN 94c + NN 100c), full match, certain; Ceres/Deneb Algedi onto natal Ketu (TN 88c + NN 97c); Rahu/Quaoar onto natal Saturn (90c / 68c); Rahu/Aldebaran onto natal Mercury (possible).
- Sheppard, other exclusive items: Jupiter/Orcus Type 1 (N 85c + X 41c) with an Orcus hub (Jupiter, Rahu); Gonggong/Vertex Type 1 (`~`); Pluto self Dec Sqrt2 81c; Gonggong selves (`~`); Mars/Bellatrix, Ketu/Castor and Vesta/Sirius natal pairs; Vesta hub (Sirius, Uranus) and Ketu hub (Castor, Uranus); Orcus/Transpluto onto natal Makemake (TN + TN, 94c / 94c, full match); Pallas/Algorab onto natal Orcus (93c / NN 75c); Chiron/Betelgeuse onto natal Uranus (TN 71c + NN 93c).

**What the pinpoint reading shows**

- One body taking three pairs: Solo's natal Quaoar (Chiron/Betelgeuse and Chiron/Sedna from the Chiron–Sedna–Betelgeuse group, plus Rahu/Aldebaran; certain, full match, exclusive; TN Chiron in two, NN star in two).
- Threads through both charts:
  - Solo/Cobden: Chiron–Sedna–Betelgeuse, transit and certain on both (the shared Chiron/Betelgeuse link and the Sedna/Betelgeuse section G T1, X on both; Betelgeuse and Sedna hubs), ending on the horse's natal Quaoar (exclusive) and on natal Betelgeuse on both charts (Chiron/Sedna, TN + TN, each side shared with Moore).
  - Cascova/Jacob: Rahu–Pluto (Rahu/Algol natal pair on both, birth chart; Pluto/Rahu X on both, transit, certain; Rahu hubs on both), landing on the horse's natal Neptune (Pluto/Bellatrix) and Jacob's natal Rahu (Vesta/Sedna), certain, exclusive, different pairs.
  - Not Available/Sheppard: Mars–Sedna–Sun–Ceres through three exclusive links, mostly birth chart and mostly possible on the horse, transit and certain on Sheppard, with no natal-body landing on either side.
  - Mister Coffey/Bowen: Ketu–Sedna, transit on the horse (X 98c) and birth chart on Bowen (N 77c and a Ketu hub); Jupiter/Orcus on both charts (horse natal Sedna, shared; Bowen natal Gonggong, exclusive).
- Chiron self in Ninths: Moore (RA Ninths 87c, sky family, certain, exclusive), the only Chiron self in the race, with Chiron and Sedna hubs and the Chiron/Sedna natal pair.
- Exclusive links at 80 or above: Solo/Cobden three (plus two section G T1s); Not Available/Sheppard three; Mister Coffey/Bowen two; Cascova/Jacob one; Fifty Ball/Moore none.

**Against the result:** 1st Not Available (14/1, Stan Sheppard); 2nd Solo (3/1, Cobden); 3rd Mister Coffey (5/4 favourite, Bowen); 4th Fifty Ball (3/1, Moore); 5th Cascova (14/1, Jacob).

- The winner has three exclusive links at 80 or above (Ceres/Mars T1 on one, Sun/Sedna, Mars/Sedna) and the section G Eris/Quaoar. They form the Mars–Sedna–Sun–Ceres thread: mostly birth chart and mostly possible on the horse (natal pairs and hubs; one transit, X Sedna→Ketu 72c), transit and certain on Sheppard (X Sedna→Sun 91c, X Sedna→Mars 94c, X Ceres→Mars 46c; an exclusive Sedna hub). The same two pairs mirror across the charts: on the horse they are natal pairs, Sedna→Sun 88p and Mars→Sedna 60p (both possible); on Sheppard, transit Sedna reaches his natal Sun (91c) and natal Mars (94c, full match), both certain, with his exclusive Sedna hub (Mars, Sun). No natal body on either chart takes those pairs. The horse's natal Jupiter takes Ketu/Uranus and Ketu/Castor (certain, full match, exclusive).
- Solo/Cobden, with the Chiron–Sedna–Betelgeuse transit thread and the race's only three-pair body (the horse's natal Quaoar), finished 2nd at 3/1.
- The identical-chart pair: Mister Coffey/Bowen 3rd (5/4 favourite), Fifty Ball/Moore 4th. Moore carried the race's only Chiron self (RA Ninths 87c, certain, exclusive).
- Cascova/Jacob, with the Rahu–Pluto thread landing on a natal body on each chart, finished last at 14/1.

"Full match" = the sky pair's own family and coordinate (**); "sky family" = same family, other coordinate (*); `~` = a match only through a secondary family scoring 65–80, read like a sub-80 pair. The Mars body they shared did not carry either to the win.
- The triangle (Mr Curiosity's natal Vesta, all possible) finished 4th.

  - **The First King/Tabti, Neptune–Haumea.** Section G only, mostly birth chart; Rahu–Ketu landings on Tabti.
  - **Mindspin/Morris, Sedna.** Silver Ratio on the horse; the link itself a common natal pair.
- The same body across partnerships (exclusive A or G links):
  - Jupiter: Mutarabby/Marquand and Mercian Hymn/Probert.
  - Neptune: The First King/Tabti (G) and Mardoof/Hornby.
  - Chiron: Ivynator/Donohoe (G) and Liberated Lad/Kingscote.
  - Sun: Liberated Lad/Kingscote (G) and Torbellino/Havlin.
  - Juno: Torbellino/Havlin and Mardoof/Hornby (G).
  - Mercury: Ivynator/Donohoe (G) and Torbellino/Havlin.
  - Pluto: Mutarabby/Marquand (G) and Mercian Hymn/Probert (G).
  - Alkaid: Ivynator/Donohoe (G) and Mardoof/Hornby.
- Chiron self in Ninths: Mardoof (Dec Ninths 68p, through a secondary family `~`, possible, exclusive), the only one in the race.
- Exclusive links at 80 or above:
  - Mutarabby/Marquand: three (plus one section G).
  - Torbellino/Havlin: two.
  - Mardoof/Hornby: two (plus one section G).
  - Ivynator/Donohoe: one (a common natal pair; plus four section G).
  - Mercian Hymn/Probert: one (plus one section G).
  - Liberated Lad/Kingscote: one (plus one section G).
  - Mindspin/Morris: one (a common natal pair).
  - The First King/Tabti: none (two section G).

**Against the result:** 1st Mercian Hymn (13/8 favourite, David Probert); 2nd Torbellino (4/1, Robert Havlin); 3rd Mardoof (33/1, Rob Hornby); 4th Mutarabby (9/1, Tom Marquand); 5th Ivynator (5/1, Stevie Donohoe); 6th The First King (11/2, Mohammed Tabti); 7th Mindspin (33/1, Luke Morris); 8th Liberated Lad (10/1, Richard Kingscote).

- The winner, the favourite, has one exclusive link and one section G link:
  - Jupiter/Bellatrix (RA Ninths 98.9), N on both: horse RA Ninths 76c (full match), Probert Dec Sqrt2 95c (other family). Birth chart, certain, on natal Jupiter on both charts, with Jupiter hubs on both (mostly N).
  - Section G Ceres/Pluto, T1 on one, Golden Ratio throughout: horse X Ceres→Pluto 55c + S Pluto 82c (transit); Probert N Ceres→Pluto 67p (birth chart), with his all-N Pluto hub (Betelgeuse, Ceres, Procyon, Quaoar).
  - No three-pair body.
- Sun and Uranus on both of the winner's charts:
  - Horse: the Sun/Uranus Type 1 (X Sun→Uranus RA Ninths 94c + S Uranus 96c), certain, full match.
  - Ceres/Rigel (RA Sqrt2, possible) lands on the horse's natal Sun (TN Ceres 60p + NN Rigel 91p) and on Probert's natal Uranus (TN Ceres 84p + NN Rigel 97p).
  - The same two bodies are reached on each chart: Ninths and certain on the horse, Sqrt2 and possible across the pair.
- The winner horse also carries the 2nd's exclusive pair: Sun/Uranus as a horse Type 1 (X Sun→Uranus RA Ninths 94c + S Uranus 96c, full match), stronger than on Torbellino's own horse (X 63c). The link is Torbellino/Havlin's, not the winner's.
- Shared body: on both of its bodies the winner held the lower pair.
  - Jupiter: Mutarabby/Marquand's Jupiter/Gonggong (99.3 against the winner's 98.9), 4th. Mutarabby's natal Jupiter also takes a Jupiter self through the winner's pair (RA Ninths 92c).
  - Pluto: Mutarabby/Marquand's section G Pluto/Quaoar (77.4 against Ceres/Pluto 69.6).
- 2nd Torbellino/Havlin: two exclusive links (Sun/Uranus T1×T1, Juno/Mercury), the Silver Ratio triangle on the horse's natal Saturn (certain), and Havlin's Silver Ratio Sun items.
- 3rd Mardoof/Hornby (33/1): two exclusive links through Neptune selves, Ninths throughout, and the race's only Chiron self in Ninths (`~`, possible).
- The partnership with the most exclusive links (Mutarabby/Marquand, three, with the Sqrt2 run) finished 4th.
- The two Silver Ratio three-pair bodies on the same Algorab–Juno pairs finished 6th (The First King) and 7th (Mindspin).
