  - Venus: Isolate/Harrison (Venus/Vesta) and Wildfire Warrior/O'Brien (Venus/Transpluto G). Vesta: Isolate/Harrison and Jimmy The Digger/Patrick (Vesta/Gonggong G).
- Chiron self in Ninths: Java Point (63c) and Ben Jones (72c), shared between them.
- Exclusive links at 80 or above:
  - Java Point/Burke: three (one a common natal pair) plus one section G.
  - Une De La Seniere/Brennan: three (two common natal pairs) plus one section G.
  - Fanamix/Jones: two (one `~`) plus one section G.
  - Wildfire Warrior/O'Brien: one (T1 on one) plus two section G.
  - Jimmy The Digger/Patrick: one (T1 on one) plus three section G.
  - Isolate/Harrison: one (T1×T1) plus one section G.
  - Method Madness/Edwards: none.

**Against the result:** 1st Java Point (2/1 favourite, Jonathan Burke); 2nd Isolate (4/1, Liam Harrison); 3rd Une De La Seniere (16/1, Paddy Brennan); 4th Fanamix (25/1, Ben Jones); 5th Jimmy The Digger (7/2, Richard Patrick); 6th Method Madness (8/1, Alex Edwards); 7th Wildfire Warrior (5/1, Tom O'Brien).

- The winner was the favourite. It has three exclusive links and one exclusive section G:
  - Ceres/Ketu (RA Ninths 97.7): X Ceres→Ketu Dec Ninths on both (57c / 48p), transit.
  - Neptune/Procyon (Dec Ninths 96.1): horse N Neptune→Procyon RA Ninths 40c (birth chart, at the floor; a common natal pair, also on Harrison, O'Brien and Patrick); Burke S Neptune RA Ninths 72c (transit, exclusive).
  - Chiron/Polaris (Dec Whole Number 91.5): horse N Chiron→Polaris Dec Sqrt2 87c (birth chart, no sky-family match); Burke X Chiron→Polaris Dec Whole Number 85c (full match, transit).
  - Section G Pallas/Haumea (77.4): possible on both.
- Family: Ceres/Ketu and Neptune/Procyon are Ninths on both sides; Chiron/Polaris is mixed (Sqrt2 on the horse's natal pair, Whole Number on Burke). Not one family throughout.
- The winner's horse holds the Chiron self in Ninths (RA Ninths 63c, full match), via the sub-80 Chiron/Alphecca (not through its own link), shared with Ben Jones (72c), whose partnership finished 4th. A Chiron-self-in-Ninths holder won, but this is closer to race 3's Toussarok (shared) than to race 18's Coleman, whose Chiron self was exclusive and inside his own link (Chiron/PoF).
- Chiron on both charts: horse Chiron hub (Algorab, Polaris) and Chiron/Algorab natal pair, exclusive; Burke Chiron hub (Orcus, Polaris) and Aldebaran/Midheaven onto his natal Chiron.
- Shared body: on Chiron and Procyon the winner held the highest pair.
  - Chiron: Fanamix/Jones's Chiron/Procyon (81.1) and Chiron/Alphecca G (79.2) against the winner's 91.5. Fanamix's horse also carries Chiron/Polaris (X 91c).
  - Procyon: the winner's Neptune/Procyon (96.1) against Une De La Seniere/Brennan's Pallas/Procyon (88.1) and Fanamix/Jones's Chiron/Procyon (81.1).
  - Pallas (G): the winner's Pallas/Haumea (77.4) is lower than Une De La Seniere/Brennan's Pallas/PoF (89.0).
  - Ketu, Ceres, Neptune, Polaris: no other exclusive link. Ceres/Ketu is a natal pair on O'Brien (92p), last.
- 2nd Isolate/Harrison: one exclusive link, Venus/Vesta T1×T1 (transit on both, Phi Powers with `~` Ninths), and section G Orcus/Transpluto. Ketu/Arcturus and Ketu/PoF land on both its charts (horse natal Juno, possible; Harrison natal Pallas, certain, full match); Ketu is a body of the winner's top link. Harrison's natal Quaoar takes four Ninths pairs.
- 3rd Une De La Seniere/Brennan, the other partnership with three exclusive links (two of them common natal pairs), plus the T1×T1 section G Transpluto/Algorab and the Pallas–Uranus–PoF triangle on Brennan's natal Ceres. The two partnerships with the most exclusive links finished 1st and 3rd.
- 4th Fanamix/Jones: two exclusive links (Chiron/Procyon X on both; Mars/Orcus `~`) and the Chiron/Alphecca section G, with the other Chiron self in Ninths.
- Wildfire Warrior/O'Brien, with the one Phi Powers-throughout Jupiter thread (T1 on one, plus two section G), finished last. Method Madness/Edwards, with no exclusive link, finished 6th.

  - Cedar Hill/Burke: two plus three section G.
  - One True King/Twiston-Davies: one (its natal pair also on Ben Jones) plus one section G.

**Against the result:** 1st Frero Banbou (9/4 favourite, Charlie Deutsch); 2nd Eclair D'Ainay (11/2, Harry Skelton); 3rd Le Ligerien (4/1, Ben Jones); 4th One True King (4/1, Sam Twiston-Davies); 5th Cedar Hill (11/1, Jonathan Burke); 6th Beaufort West (20/1, Page Fuller); Financier (13/2, Richard Patrick) pulled up.

- The winner was the favourite. It has four exclusive links and no exclusive section G:
  - Eris/Procyon (Dec Ninths 92.1), T1 on one: horse N Eris→Procyon Dec Whole Number 87c (a common natal pair) + S Eris Dec Ninths 48c (full match); Deutsch S Eris RA Ninths 45c.
  - Eris/Castor (Dec Ninths 95.9): S Eris on both (48c / 45c), the same selves.
  - Sun/Vesta (Dec Ninths 88.5): horse X Vesta→Sun RA Ninths 82c (transit); Deutsch N Sun→Vesta RA Ninths 75p (exclusive natal pair).
  - Saturn/Quaoar (RA Golden Ratio 97.1): N on both (96c / 53c), a common natal pair (also on Twiston-Davies).
- Family: every transit item on the three Eris and Sun/Vesta links is Ninths; the two natal pairs (Eris/Procyon on the horse, Saturn/Quaoar on both) are not.
- Its thread is Eris on both charts: Eris selves on both, Eris/Castor on the horse's natal Jupiter and Deutsch's natal Uranus (birth chart, certain), the horse's Eris hub (Algorab, Procyon, Vertex).
- On the horse, the top pair Venus/Arcturus (100.0) as an exclusive Type 1 (N 75c + S Venus 52c, Phi Powers), and Mercury/Castor Type 1 with the Mercury self 99c.
- Moon/Pluto, Financier's exclusive link, lands on both of the winner's charts.
- Shared body:
  - Eris: the winner held the highest pairs (95.9, 92.1) against Beaufort West/Fuller's section G Eris/Polaris (78.1).
  - Castor: the winner's Eris/Castor (95.9) against Le Ligerien/Jones's Castor/PoS (95.4, 3rd) and Cedar Hill/Burke's section G Saturn/Castor (74.1).
  - Saturn: the winner's Saturn/Quaoar (97.1) is the highest Saturn link.
  - Vesta: the winner's Sun/Vesta (88.5) is below the 2nd, Eclair D'Ainay/Skelton's Rahu/Vesta (89.7), and above Beaufort West/Fuller's Mars/Vesta (80.4).
- No Chiron self on the winner. The only Chiron self in Ninths was Ben Jones's (93c, exclusive, via the non-link Chiron/Alphecca), whose partnership finished 3rd. Saturn/Quaoar, the winner's link, lands on both Le Ligerien/Jones charts, and Le Ligerien carries Eris/Castor as a natal pair (94c).
- 2nd Eclair D'Ainay/Skelton: three exclusive links on the Chiron–Haumea–Mars triangle and Rahu/Vesta (none common), two section G, and the whole triangle on the horse's natal Rahu. The winner's Eris/Castor lands on Skelton's natal Ketu (TN 78c + NN 89c).
- The Chiron–Haumea–Mars triangle landed whole on Eclair D'Ainay (2nd), One True King (4th, highest scores), Beaufort West (6th) and Patrick (pulled up). The winner's charts carry Chiron/Haumea only on the horse's natal Pluto (70c / 70c), the same pair on the same natal body as One True King's (91c / 92c).
- The four-link partnerships finished 1st (Frero Banbou/Deutsch), 6th (Beaufort West/Fuller, plus two section G) and pulled up (Financier/Patrick).
